# Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin

ברטנורא על משנה גיטין

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin)

חיבור התשתית הקדום של חז״ל, המתעד מגוון דעות הלכתיות ממסורת התורה שבעל פה. נחתם בשנת 200 לספירה לערך.

16th-century commentary printed in most editions of the Mishnah, including summaries of talmudic discussions and legal conclusions.

Sections: Chapter-Mishnah-Comment

Location: Mishnah/Rishonim on Mishnah/Bartenura/Seder Nashim

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (He): Torat-Emet (Segments: 339, License: CC-BY-NC)

Version (En): Bartenura on Mishnah, trans. by Rabbi Robert Alpert, 2020 (Segments: 336)


## Chapter 1


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 1:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:1:1)


**הַמֵּבִיא גֵט מִמְּדִינַת הַיָּם.** כָּל חוּצָה לָאֶרֶץ קָרֵי מְדִינַת הַיָּם:


המביא גט ממדינת הים – All [areas] that are outside of the land of Israel are called, “countries aboard.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 1:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:1:2)


**צָרִיךְ לוֹמַר בְּפָנַי נִכְתַּב וּבְפָנַי נֶחְתָּם.** אִית דְּאָמְרֵי טַעְמָא לְפִי שֶׁאֵין בְּנֵי מְדִינַת הַיָּם בְּנֵי תוֹרָה, וְאֵין יוֹדְעִין שֶׁצָּרִיךְ לִכְתֹּב הַגֵּט לְשֵׁם הָאִשָּׁה, הִלְכָּךְ אוֹמֵר הַשָּׁלִיחַ בְּפָנַי נִכְתַּב וּבְפָנַי נֶחְתָּם, וּמִמֵּילָא שָׁיְלִינַן לֵיהּ אִם נִכְתַּב לִשְׁמָהּ, וְהוּא אוֹמֵר אִין. וְאִית דְּאָמְרֵי טַעְמָא לְפִי שֶׁאֵין שַׁיָּרוֹת מְצוּיוֹת מִשָּׁם לְכָאן, שֶׁאִם יָבֹא הַבַּעַל וִיעַרְעֵר לוֹמַר לֹא כְתַבְתִּיו שֶׁיִּהְיוּ עֵדִים מְצוּיִין לְהַכִּיר חֲתִימַת הָעֵדִים. וְהֶאֱמִינוּהוּ רַבָּנָן לַשָּׁלִיחַ כְּבִתְרֵי, וְשׁוּב לֹא יוֹעִיל עִרְעוּר הַבַּעַל:


צריך לומר בפני נכתב ובפני נחתם – There are those who give the [following] reason, because there aren’t Torah scholars found in countries abroad, and they don’t know that one must write the Jewish bill of divorce for [the express purpose of] the [specific] woman. Therefore, the agent states, “it was written in my presence and it was signed in my presence.” And of itself, we ask him [the agent] if it [the Jewish bill of divorce] was written for her sake [alone], and he answers in the affirmative; [on the other hand,] there are those who say that the reason [that he must say that the Jewish bill of divorce was written in his presence and signed in his presence], because caravans [bearing numbers of people] are not found [traveling] from there to here, for if the husband would coביןme and raise a complaint [contesting the legality of the action] saying: “I did not write it,” witnesses would be found to recognize the signatures of those who witnessed [the writing of the Jewish bill of divorce]. But the Rabbis believed the agent as if he were two [witnesses], and furthermore, the complaint of the husband would have no effect.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 1:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:1:3)


**מִן הָרֶקֶם וּמִן הַחֶגֶר.** בֵּין קָדֵשׁ וּבֵין בָּרֶד, מְתַרְגְמִינַן בֵּין רֶקֶם וּבֵין חִגְרָא:


בין קדש ובין ברד – (As is written in Genesis 16:14): “[Therefore the well was called Beer-lahai-roi;] it is between Kadesh and Bered.” And the Aramaic (Onkelos) translation of this is: "מן הרקם ומן החגר" .


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 1:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:1:4)


**אֲפִלּוּ מִכְּפַר לוּדִים.** שֶׁהִיא מִחוּצָה לָאָרֶץ:


אפילו מכפר לודים – which is considered outside of the land of Israel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 1:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:1:5)


**לְלוֹד.** שֶׁהִיא סְמוּכָה לָהּ וְהִיא מֵאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל:


ללוד – which is near it and it is from the land of Israel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 1:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:1:6)


**מֵהֶגְמוֹנְיָא לְהֶגְמוֹנְיָא.** שְׁנֵי הֶגְמוֹנִים בְּעִיר אַחַת וּמַקְפִּידִים זֶה עַל זֶה:


מהגמוניא להגמוניא – there were in one town two jurisdictions jealous of each other (Gittin 4a).



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 1:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:2:1)


**מֵרֶקֶם לַמִּזְרָח.** מֵרֶקֶם עַד סוֹף הָעוֹלָם לְמִזְרָחוֹ קָרוּי חוּץ לָאָרֶץ, וְרֶקֶם עַצְמָהּ נִדּוֹנָה כְּמִזְרַח הָעוֹלָם וְלֹא כְאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל:


מרקם למזרח – from Rekem until the end of the world to its east is called outside the land of Israel. But Rekem itself is judged as being in the eastern part of the world and not in the land of Israel.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 1:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:3:1)


**וְאִם יֵשׁ עָלָיו עוֹרְרִים.** שֶׁהַבַּעַל מְעַרְעֵר שֶׁהוּא מְזֻיָּף:


ואם יש עליו עוררים – that the husband complains that it [the Jewish bill of divorce] is forged.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 1:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:3:2)


**יִתְקַיֵּם בְּחוֹתְמָיו.** אִם יָעִידוּ הָעֵדִים עַל חֲתִימַת יְדֵיהֶם, אוֹ עֵדִים אֲחֵרִים יַכִּירוּ חֲתִימָתָם, כָּשֵׁר. וּבַזְּמַן הַזֶּה, הַמֵּבִיא גֵּט בֵּין בְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל בֵּין בְּחוּץ לָאָרֶץ צָרִיךְ לִתְּנוֹ לָהּ בִּפְנֵי שְׁנַיִם וְצָרִיךְ לוֹמַר בְּפָנַי נִכְתַּב וּבְפָנַי נֶחְתָּם. וְאִם חֲתִימַת הָעֵדִים נִכֶּרֶת בִּמְקוֹם נְתִינַת הַגֵּט וְנִתְקַיֵּם הַגֵּט בְּחוֹתְמָיו, אֵין צָרִיךְ לוֹמַר בְּפָנַי נִכְתַּב וּבְפָנַי נֶחְתָּם:


יתקיים בחותמיו – if the witnesses will testify on their own signatures, or other witnesses who recognize their signatures [of the original witnesses], it is valid. And in our times, a person who brings a Jewish bill of divorce whether from the land of Israel or from outside the land of Israel must give it to her [the wife] in the presence of two [witnesses] and must say: ”It was written my presence and signed in my presence.” And if the signatures of the witnesses are recognized in the location where the Jewish bill of divorce is delivered and the Jewish bill of divorce is validated through its signatories, there is no need to say: “it was written in my presence and signed in my presence.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 1:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:3:3)


**וְאֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לוֹמַר.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁנְּתָנוֹ לָהּ כְּשֶׁהוּא פִּקֵּחַ וְלֹא הִסְפִּיק לוֹמַר בְּפָנַי נִכְתַּב וּבְפָנַי נֶחְתָּם עַד שֶׁנִּתְחָרֵשׁ:


ואינו יכול לומר – [it was written in my presence and signed in my presence] for example, that it [the Jewish bill of divorce] was given to her when he was lucid but he was not able to say: “it was written in my presence and signed in my presence” until he became a deaf-mute.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 1:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:4:1)


**שָׁוִין לַמּוֹלִיךְ וְלַמֵּבִיא.** לוֹמַר בְּפָנַי נִכְתַּב וּבְפָנַי נֶחְתָּם:


שוין למוליך ומביא – to state “it was written in my presence and signed in my presence.”



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 1:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:5:1)


**חוּץ מִגִּטֵּי נָשִׁים.** שֶׁאִם יֵשׁ עָלָיו עֵד כּוּתִי אֶחָד, כָּשֵׁר. אֲבָל שְׁנֵיהֶם כּוּתִים, פּוֹסֵל תַּנָּא קַמָּא אֲפִלּוּ בְּגִטֵּי נָשִׁים. וְרַבָּן (שמעון בן) גַּמְלִיאֵל עָשָׂה מַעֲשֶׂה וְהִכְשִׁיר אֲפִלּוּ כְּשֶׁשְּׁנֵיהֶם כּוּתִים. וְהָאִידְנָא דְּגָזְרוּ עַל הַכּוּתִים שֶׁיִּהְיוּ כְּגוֹיִם לְכָל דִּבְרֵיהֶם, לֹא שְׁנָא שְׁאָר שְׁטָרוֹת וְלֹא שְׁנָא גִּטֵּי נָשִׁים, אֲפִלּוּ עֵד אֶחָד כּוּתִי, פָּסוּל:


חוץ מגיטי נשים – that if there was upon it [a Jewish bill of divorce] one witnesses who is a Cuthean/a member of the sect of the Samaritans (on occasion, this word is used in place of גוי/non-Jew; עכו"ם /idol worshiper or מין/sectarian as a result of censorship), it is valid, if both of the them are Cutheans, the first teacher of the Mishnah disqualifies them for Jewish bills of divorce, but Rabban Gamaliel relates a story where he validated [it], even where both of them [the witnesses] were Cutheans. But nowadays, they [the Rabbis] decreed [against] the Cutheans as they were [to be considered] as non-Jews in all matters, making no distinction between other documents and Jewish bills of divorce, for even if one witness was a Cuthean, it [the document] is invalid.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 1:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:5:2)


**בְּעַרְכָּאוֹת שֶׁל גּוֹיִם.** שֶׁהֵעִידוּ הָעֵדִים עֵדוּתָם לִפְנֵי הַשּׁוֹפֵט בִּמְקוֹם מוֹשַׁב מִשְׁפְּטֵיהֶם. וְהוּא שֶׁיָּדַעְנוּ בְּאוֹתוֹ שׁוֹפֵט וּבְאוֹתָם עֵדִים דְּלֹא מְקַבְּלֵי שֹׁחֲדָא. וְדַוְקָא בְּשִׁטְרֵי הַלְוָאוֹת וְשִׁטְרֵי מִקָּח וּמִמְכָּר, שֶׁהָעֵדִים רָאוּ בִּנְתִינַת הַמָּמוֹן. אֲבָל שִׁטְרֵי הוֹדָאוֹת וְגִטֵּי נָשִׁים, וְכָל דָּבָר שֶׁהוּא מַעֲשֵׂה בֵּית דִּין בְּעַרְכָּאוֹת שֶׁלָּהֶם, הַכֹּל פָּסוּל:


בערכאות של גוים – that the witnesses would offer their testimony before the sitting judge in the place where their matters of justice take place. But this is where they knew that judge and of those witnesses who do not accept bribes. And this specifically [refers to] loan documents and documents of sale where the witnesses saw funds exchanged. But documents of admission and Jewish bills of divorce, and any thing which is a legal matter in their [i.e., Roman] gentile offices, everything is invalid.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 1:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:5:3)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אוֹמֵר כְּשֵׁרִים.** לֹא הֻזְכְּרוּ בְּבֵית הַמִּדְרָשׁ לְהִפָּסֵל:


רבי שמעון אומר כשרים – they were not mentioned in the Jewish academy (Bet Midrash) to be invalidated.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 1:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:5:4)


**אֶלָּא בִזְמַן שֶׁנַּעֲשׂוּ בְהֶדְיוֹט.** עַל יְדֵי גּוֹיִם הֶדְיוֹטוֹת שֶׁאֵינָם דַּיָּנִים. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


אלא בזמן שנעשו בהדיוט – through non-Jewish commoners who are not judges, but the Halakha does not follow like [the opinion of] Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 1:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:6:1)


**רָצָה לַחֲזֹר בִּשְׁנֵיהֶן.** קֹדֶם שֶׁיַּגִּיעַ לְיַד הָאִשָּׁה וְהָעֶבֶד:


רצה לחזור בשניהן – before it [the Jewish bill of divorce or the bill of manumission] reaches the hand of the woman and/or the slave.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 1:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:6:2)


**יַחֲזֹר.** וְאֵין הַשָּׁלִיחַ יָכוֹל לִזְכּוֹת בָּהֶם לְצָרְכָן, דְּחוֹב הוּא לָהֶן, שֶׁמְאַבְּדִין מְזוֹנוֹתֵיהֶן:


יחזור – But the agent cannot provide their needs for their benefit since it [the document] is something to their detriment and they lose their food/support.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 1:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:6:3)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים בְּגִטֵּי נָשִׁים.** יָכוֹל לַחֲזֹר:


וחכמים אומרים: בגיטי נשים – He (i.e., the husband) can retract (his Jewish bill of divorce),


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 1:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:6:4)


**אֲבָל לֹא בְשִׁחְרוּרֵי עֲבָדִים.** וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


אבל לא בשחרורי עבדים – but he cannot retract in his bill of manumission of slaves. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 1:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:6:5)


**שֶׁאִם יִרְצֶה שֶׁלֹּא לָזוּן אֶת עַבְדּוֹ רַשַּׁאי.** הִלְכָּךְ כִּי מְשַׁחְרֵר לֵיהּ לֹא מַפְסִיד לֵיהּ מְזוֹנֵי. אֲבָל שֶׁלֹּא לָזוּן אֶת אִשְׁתּוֹ אֵינוֹ רַשַּׁאי, הִלְכָּךְ כִּי מְגָרֵשׁ לָהּ מַפְסִיד לָהּ מְזוֹנֵי:


שאם ירצה שלא לזון את עבדו רשאי – Therefore, when he (i.e., the owner) frees him (i.e., his slave), he does not cause him to lose his support/food, but he is not permitted to feed his wife; therefore, if he divorces her, she causes her to lose her support/food.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 1:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:6:6)


**מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהוּא קִנְיָנוֹ.** כְּלוֹמַר הָא דְּהוּא אָכִיל בַּתְּרוּמָה כְּשֶׁהוּא עֶבֶד כֹּהֵן אֵינוֹ אֶלָּא מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהוּא קִנְיָנוֹ שֶׁל כֹּהֵן, מִידֵי דַּהֲוָה אַבְּהֶמְתּוֹ שֶׁל כֹּהֵן שֶׁאוֹכֶלֶת בְּכַרְשִׁינֵי תְרוּמָה, וְלָאו מִשּׁוּם מְעַלְּיוּתָא הִיא, הִלְכָּךְ כִּי מְשַׁחְרֵר לֵיהּ אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁמַּפְסִידוֹ מִלֶּאֱכֹל בַּתְּרוּמָה אֵין זוֹ חוֹבָה לָעֶבֶד:


מפני שהוא קנינו – That is to say, since he {i.e., the slave] eats the heave-offering/Terumah when the slave is the servant of a Kohen/Priest, it is only because he is the purchase-property of the Kohen/Priest, since regarding the cattle of Kohen/Priest which can eat the veches of Terumah/heave-offering, but it is not a a perfect comparison. Therefore, when he frees him (i.e. the slave), even though he causes him to lose the ability to eat Terumah/heave-offering, it is not an obligation for the slave [to do so].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 1:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:6:7)


**לֹא יִתְּנוּ לְאַחַר מִיתָה.** דְּגִּטָּא לֹא הָוֵי עַד דְּמָטֵי לְיָדָהּ וְכִי מָטֵי לְיָדָהּ הָא מֵת וְאֵין גֵּט לְאַחַר מִיתָה. וּשְׁטַר שִׁחְרוּר נַמִּי כִּי מְטָא לְיָדֵיהּ הָא מֵת וּפָקַע רְשׁוּתֵיהּ מִנֵּיהּ:


לא יתנו לאחר מיתה – The Get/Jewish bill of divorce does not take effect until it reaches her hand, and if it reaches her hand, he has died, and there is no Jewish bill of divorce after death. And similarly, the document of manumission, when it reaches his (i.e. the slave’s) hand if he died, his ownership over him is broken.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 1:6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/1:6:8)


**יִתְּנוּ לְאַחַר מִיתָה.** וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּלֹא אָמַר מָנֶה זֶה, דְּדִבְרֵי שְׁכִיב מֵרַע כִּכְתוּבִין וְכִמְסוּרִין דָּמוּ:


יתנו לאחר מיתה – even though he (i.e., the now deceased owner) did not say, “this Maneh”/weight equivalent to fifty silver shekels, since the words of someone who is on his death bed are regarded as if they are written and transmitted.



## Chapter 2


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 2:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:1:1)


**הַמֵּבִיא.** נֶחְתַּם חֶצְיוֹ. אֶחָד מִן הָעֵדִים חָתַם בְּפָנַי:


המביא – נחתם חציו – one of the witnesses signed in my presence.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 2:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:1:2)


**בְּפָנַי נִכְתַּב חֶצְיוֹ וְכוּ'‏ פָּסוּל.** וְדַוְקָא חֶצְיוֹ הָאַחֲרוֹן. אֲבָל בְּפָנַי נִכְתַּב חֶצְיוֹ הָרִאשׁוֹן שֶׁהוּא שֵׁם הָאִישׁ וְהָאִשָּׁה וְהַזְּמַן, כָּשֵׁר. וְחֶצְיוֹ הָרִאשׁוֹן נַמִּי לֹא צָרִיךְ שֶׁיִּרְאֶה הַכְּתִיבָה עַצְמָהּ, אֶלָּא אִם שָׁמַע קוֹל הַקֻּלְמוֹס בִּלְבַד עוֹבֵר עַל הַנְּיָר בִּשְׁעַת כְּתִיבָה, תּוּ לֹא צָרִיךְ:


נכתב חציו וכו' פסול – and specifically the final half. But [if the agent said}: “in my presence, the first-half was written, which is the [the section containing the] name of the man and [the name of] the woman and the time (i.e. date), it is valid. And [regarding] the first half as well, it is not necessary for him [the agent] to see the writing itself, but if he heard the sound of the reed/writing pen alone passing over the paper at the time of the writing [of the Jewish bill of divorce], nothing further is necessary.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 2:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:1:3)


**אֶחָד אוֹמֵר בְּפָנַי נִכְתָּב וְאֶחָד אוֹמֵר בְּפָנַי נֶחְתָּם פָּסוּל.** בִּזְמַן שֶׁהַגֵּט יוֹצֵא מִתַּחַת יַד אֶחָד מֵהֶם, דְּאַצְרְכוּהוּ רַבָּנָן לְשָׁלִיחַ הַמֵּבִיא גֵּט לְמֵימַר תַּרְוַיְהוּ. אֲבָל אִם הַגֵּט יוֹצֵא מִתַּחַת יְדֵי שְׁנֵיהֶם, כָּשֵׁר, שֶׁשְּׁנַיִם שֶׁהֵבִיאוּ גֵּט אֵין צָרִיךְ שֶׁיֹּאמְרוּ בְּפָנֵינוּ נִכְתַּב וּבְפָנֵינוּ נֶחְתַּם:


אחד אומר בפני נכתב ואחד אומר בפני נחתם פסול – at the time when the Jewish bill of divorce leaves from under the hand of one of them (i.e., the agents), and since the Rabbis required for the agent who brings the Jewish bill of divorce to say both [things] (i.e. that the Get was written in my presence and that the Get was signed in my presence), but if the Jewish bill of divorce leaves the hands of both of them (i.e., the agents), it is considered valid. For two [individuals] who brought a Jewish bill of divorce, they do not need to say, “in our presence it was written and in our presence it was signed.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 2:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:1:4)


**שְׁנַיִם אוֹמְרִים בְּפָנֵינוּ נִכְתָּב וְאֶחָד אוֹמֵר בְּפָנַי נֶחְתָּם פָּסוּל.** בִּזְמַן שֶׁהַגֵּט יוֹצֵא מִתַּחַת יַד אֶחָד מֵהֶם. אֲבָל אִם יוֹצֵא מִתַּחַת יַד שְׁנֵיהֶם, כָּשֵׁר:


שנים אומרים בפנינו נכתב ואחד אומר בפני נחתם פסול – At the time when the Jewish bill of divorce leaves under the hand of one of them, but if it leaves the hand of both of them, it is valid.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 2:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:1:5)


**וְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה מַכְשִׁיר.** וַאֲפִלּוּ יוֹצֵא מִתַּחַת יָדוֹ שֶׁל אֶחָד מֵהֶם. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


ור' יהודה מכשיר – And even if the Jewish bill of divorce leaves the hand of one of them, and the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 2:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:2:1)


**בַּלַּיְלָה וְנֶחְתַּם בַּיּוֹם כָּשֵׁר.** שֶׁהַיּוֹם הוֹלֵךְ אַחַר הַלַּיְלָה וְאֵין זֶה מֻקְדָּם:


בלילה ונחתם ביום כשר – since the daytime goes after the [previous] night, and this is not in advance/early.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 2:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:2:2)


**בַּיּוֹם וְנֶחְתַּם בַּלַּיְלָה פָּסוּל.** שֶׁהֲרֵי מֻקְדָּם הוּא. וְתִקְּנוּ חֲכָמִים זְמַן בְּגִטִּין, גְּזֵרָה שֶׁמָּא יִהְיֶה נָשׂוּי עִם בַּת אֲחוֹתוֹ וְתִזְנֶה עָלָיו, וְחָס עָלֶיהָ שֶׁלֹּא תֵּחָנֵק וְנוֹתֵן לָהּ גֵּט בְּלֹא זְמַן, וּכְשֶׁמְּעִידִים עָלֶיהָ בְּבֵית דִּין מוֹצִיאָה גִּטָּהּ וְאוֹמֶרֶת גְּרוּשָׁה הָיִיתִי וּפְנוּיָה בְּאוֹתָהּ שָׁעָה:


ביום ונחתם בלילה פסול – since it is early. And the Sages instituted the time in Jewish bills of divorce, decreed, less a person would be married with his sister’s daughter (niece}, and she will commit an offense against him, and he has compassion upon her that she not be strangled to death, and he gives her a Jewish bill of divorce without the time included, and when they testify against her in the Jewish court, she takes out her Jewish bill of divorce and states that she was a divorcee and was a free (literally, “open”) woman at that time.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 2:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:2:3)


**וְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן מַכְשִׁיר.** דְּסָבַר רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן דַּחֲכָמִים תִּקְּנוּ זְמַן בְּגִטִּין מִשּׁוּם פֵּירֵי, שֶׁאִם לֹא יִהְיֶה זְמַן בַּגֵּט יִהְיֶה הַבַּעַל מוֹכֵר וְהוֹלֵךְ פֵּרוֹת נִכְסֵי מְלוֹג שֶׁל אִשְׁתּוֹ לְאַחַר גֵּרוּשִׁין, וּכְשֶׁתִּתְבְּעוֹ בַּדִּין יֹאמַר קֹדֶם גֵּרוּשִׁין מָכַרְתִּי. וּלְהָכִי מַכְשִׁיר רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן בְּנִכְתַּב בַּיּוֹם וְנֶחְתַּם בַּלַּיְלָה אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהוּא מֻקְדָּם, דְּסָבַר מִשָּׁעָה שֶׁנָּתַן עֵינָיו לְגָרֵשׁ אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא גֵּרֵשׁ שׁוּב אֵין לוֹ לַבַּעַל פֵּרוֹת. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


ור"ש מכשיר – (a Jewish bill of divorce written in the daytime and signed at night), for Rabbi Shimon thinks (i.e., “holds”) that the Sages established [putting] the time in the Jewish bill of divorce because of the usufruct [involved], for if the time would not be [stated] in the Jewish bill of divorce, the husband would sell the usufruct (the wife’s estate of which the husband has fruition without responsibility for loss or deterioration – while married] of his wife after the divorce, and when she would claim against him in court, he would claim that he sold it prior to the divorce. And Rabbi Shimon validates this when it [the Jewish bill of divorce] was written during the day time and signed at night, even though it is in advance. For he holds that from the time that he desired to divorce her even though he had not [yet] divorced her, from then on, the husband does not have [access to] the usufruct, but the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 2:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:3:1)


**בִּדְיוֹ.** אלמיד"ר בַּעֲרָבִי:


בדיו – “Almidar” in Arabic


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 2:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:3:2)


**סִקְרָא.** צֶבַע אָדֹם:


סיקרא – Red color


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 2:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:3:3)


**קוֹמוֹס.** שְׂרַף הָאִילָן:


קומוס – resin/sap of the tree


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 2:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:3:4)


**קַנְקַנְתּוֹם.** וידריאול"ו בְּלַעַ"ז:


קנקנתום – Vidriolov in foreign tongue


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 2:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:3:5)


**עַל הֶעָלֶה שֶׁל זַיִת.** תְּלוּשָׁה:


על העלה של זית – detached


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 2:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:3:6)


**וְנוֹתֵן לָהּ אֶת הַפָּרָה.** שֶׁאֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לְקַצְּצוֹ אַחַר כְּתִיבָה, דִּכְתִיב (דברים כד) וְכָתַב לָהּ סֵפֶר כְּרִיתוּת וְנָתַן בְּיָדָהּ, מִי שֶׁאֵינוֹ מְחֻסָּר אֶלָּא כְּתִיבָה וּנְתִינָה, יָצָא זֶה שֶׁמְּחֻסָּר כְּתִיבָה קְצִיצָה וּנְתִינָה:


ונותן לה את הפרה – as he is not able to cut it after it is written, as it is written (Deuteronomy 24:3); “[then this latter man rejects her,] writes her a bill of divorcement, hands it to her [and sends her away from his house;…” which is not requiring other than writing and giving [the Jewish bill of divorce], excluding the situation which is requiring the writing [of the Jewish bill of divorce], cutting it [from where it was written] and giving it [to the woman].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 2:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:3:7)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי הַגְּלִילִי אוֹמֵר כוּ'.** דְּרַחֲמָנָא קַרְיֵהּ לְגֵט סֵפֶר, מַה סֵּפֶר מְיֻחָד שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ רוּחַ חַיִּים וְאֵינוֹ אוֹכֵל, אַף כָּל שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ רוּחַ חַיִּים וְאֵינוֹ אוֹכֵל. וְרַבָּנָן אָמְרֵי, אִי כָּתַב בַּסֵּפֶר כִּדְקְאָמַרְתְּ, הַשְׁתָּא דִּכְתִיב סֵפֶר, לִסְפִירַת דְּבָרִים הוּא דַּאֲתָא. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


רבי יוסי הגלילי אומר וכו' – Since the All-Merciful (i.e., the Torah) refers to a Jewish bill of a divorce as a ספר/bill [of divorcement] (Deuteronomy 25:1, 3); just as a bill (book) is unique and has no life-spirit in it and does not eat, so too everything which has no life-spirit in it and does not eat. And the Rabbis state that if he wrote it [the Jewish bill of divorce] in a document as you stated, here where it the word "ספר"/bill – it comes [to refer not to the writing material but merely to] the act of writing the words (formula of divorce (see Gittin 21b). And the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 2:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:4:1)


**אֵין כּוֹתְבִין בִּמְחֻבָּר.** מִפְּנֵי שֶׁמְּחֻסָּר קְצִיצָה:


אין כותבין במחובר – because it is missing “cutting.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 2:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:4:2)


**כְּתָבוֹ עַל הַמְּחֻבָּר וְכוּ'.** הָכִי קָאָמַר, כָּתַב בִּמְחֻבָּר הַטֹּפֶס, שֶׁהוּא כָּל הַגֵּט כֻּלּוֹ חוּץ מִמְּקוֹם הָאִישׁ וּמְקוֹם הָאִשָּׁה וְהַזְּמַן:


כתבו על המחובר וכו' – This is how it should be said: If he wrote [the Jewish bill of the divorce] the blank form of the document [of the Jewish bill of divorce] on something attached, which is the entire Jewish bill of divorce except for the place of the man and the place of the woman and the time (i.e., date).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 2:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:4:3)


**וּתְלָשׁוֹ וַחֲתָמוֹ.** כְּלוֹמַר שֶׁכָּתַב הַתּוֹרֵף שֶׁהוּא מְקוֹם הָאִישׁ וְהָאִשָּׁה וְהַזְּמַן אַחַר שֶׁתְּלָשׁוֹ:


תלשו וחתמו – that is to say, that he [the scribe] wrote that part of the document that makes it binding, which is the place of the man and the place of the woman and the time (i.e., date).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 2:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:4:4)


**כָּשֵׁר.** דְּכֵיוָן שֶׁנִּכְתַּב הַתּוֹרֵף בְּתָלוּשׁ, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהַטֹּפֶס נִכְתַּב בִּמְחֻבָּר, כָּשֵׁר:


כשר – For since the part of the document that makes it binding was written on [something] detached, even though the blank form of the document was written while it was attached, it is valid.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 2:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:4:5)


**עַל הַנְּיָר הַמָּחוּק.** שֶׁיָּכוֹל לַחֲזֹר וּלְמוֹחֲקוֹ עַד הָעֵדִים וְלִכְתֹּב עָלָיו מַה שֶּׁיִּרְצֶה, וְלֹא מִנַּכְּרָא מִלְּתָא, שֶׁהֲרֵי עֵדִים נַמִּי עַל הַמָּחוּק הֵם חֲתוּמִים:


על נייר המחוק – because he can go back and erase it until [the names of the witnesses] and write upon it what he wants and no one would recognize anything, for the signature of the witnesses is written over an erasure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 2:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:4:6)


**וְדִפְתְּרָא.** אֵין הַמָּחוּק שֶׁלּוֹ נִכָּר. דִּפְתְּרָא, דְּמָלִיחַ וְקָמִיחַ וְלֹא עָפִיץ:


דיפתרא – its erasure is not recognized. דיפתרא – that is, [prepared] with salt and flour, but not prepared with gall-nut juice.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 2:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:4:7)


**וַחֲכָמִים מַכְשִׁירִין.** בְּגִטִּין בִּלְבַד. דִּסְבִירָא לְהוּ עֵדֵי מְסִירָה כָּרְתֵי, וְהָעֵדִים שֶׁהַגֵּט נִמְסָר בִּפְנֵיהֶם הֵם עִקַּר הַגֵּרוּשִׁין, לֹא עֵדֵי חֲתִימָה. אֲבָל בִּשְׁאָר שְׁטָרוֹת דְּסַמְכִינַן אַעֵדֵי חֲתִימָה, מוֹדִים חֲכָמִים שֶׁאֵין נִכְתָּבִים לֹא עַל נְיָר מָחוּק וְלֹא עַל דִּפְתְּרָא. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכמים מכשירין – [The Sages validate] only in Jewish bills of divorce alone, as they think that the witnesses who deliver the Jewish bill of divorce [to the woman] make it [the divorce] final. For the Jewish bill of divorce that is delivered in the presence of the witnesses is the essence of the Jewish divorce, and not the witnesses who signed it [i.e., the Jewish bill of divorce]. But on other documents, we rely upon the witnesses who signed it [i.e., the document]. The Sages admit that it [the Jewish bill of divorce] cannot be written either on a blotted-out paper or on something prepared with gall-nut juice. And the Halakha is according the Sages.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 2:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:5:1)


**וַאֲפִלּוּ חֵרֵשׁ שׁוֹטֶה וְקָטָן.** וְהוּא שֶׁגָּדוֹל עוֹמֵד עַל גַּבָּיו וְאוֹמֵר לוֹ כְּתֹב לְשֵׁם פְּלוֹנִי. אֲבָל נָכְרִי וְעֶבֶד, אֲפִלּוּ גָּדוֹל עוֹמֵד עַל גַּבָּיו, לְכַתְּחִלָּה לֹא יִכְתֹּב גֵּט, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהֵם בְּנֵי דַעַת וְאַדַּעְתָּא דְּנַפְשַׁיְהוּ עָבְדֵי, שֶׁאֲפִלּוּ גָּדוֹל אוֹמֵר לוֹ כְּתֹב לְשֵׁם פְּלוֹנִי הוּא לֹא יִכְתֹּב אֶלָּא לְדַעַת עַצְמוֹ. וְאִם נָכְרִי וְעֶבֶד כָּתְבוּ טֹפֶס הַגֵּט, וְיִשְׂרָאֵל בֶּן דַּעַת כָּתַב הַתּוֹרֵף שֶׁהוּא שֵׁם הָאִישׁ וְהָאִשָּׁה וְהַזְּמַן, שֶׁכָּל אֵלּוּ צְרִיכִין לִשְׁמָן, הַגֵּט כָּשֵׁר. וְכֵן חֵרֵשׁ שׁוֹטֶה וְקָטָן דְּאָמְרִינַן בְּמַתְנִיתִין שֶׁכְּשֵׁרִים לִכְתֹּב אֶת הַגֵּט, הָנֵי מִלֵּי טֹפֶס, אֲבָל תּוֹרֵף אֵינוֹ כָּשֵׁר אֶלָּא שֶׁיִּכְתְּבֶנּוּ יִשְׂרָאֵל גָּדוֹל וּבֶן דַּעַת:


ואפילו חרש שוטה וקטן – [and concerning them – the deaf-mute, imbecile and minor], a Jewish adult stands over them and says to him: Write this for the sake of “so-and-so.” But a heathen and a slave, even if an adult Jew stands over them, ab initio, he should not write the Jewish bill of divorce, because they have the temperament [to write a Jewish bill of divorce], and on their personal knowledge we operate, for even if a Jewish adult says to him: “Write for the sake of “so-and-so” , he should not write other than for himself. But if a heathen or slave wrote the blank form of the document [of the Jewish bill of divorce] and a Jew possessing awareness wrote the part of the document that makes it binding; which is the name of the man and the woman and the time (i.e., date), for all of these require [being written] for their sake (i.e., the man, the woman and the Jewish bill of divorce), the Jewish bill of divorce is valid. And similarly, a when we say in our Mishnah that a deaf-mute, imbecile and/or minor are fit to write a Jewish bill of divorce, these words refer to the blank form of the document, but not the part of the document that makes it binding, is not valid, other than if it is written by an adult Jew who possesses awareness.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 2:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:5:2)


**חוּץ מֵחֵרֵשׁ שׁוֹטֶה וְקָטָן.** דְּלָאו בְּנֵי דֵעָה נִינְהוּ:


חוץ מחרש שוטה וקטן – who all lack awareness.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 2:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:5:3)


**וְסוּמָא.** פָּסוּל לְהָבִיא גֵּט מֵחוּצָה לָאָרֶץ, שֶׁאֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לוֹמַר בְּפָנַי נִכְתַּב וּבְפָנַי נֶחְתָּם. אֲבָל לְהָבִיא גֵּט בְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁאֵין צָרִיךְ לוֹמַר בְּפָנַי נִכְתַּב וּבְפָנַי נֶחְתָּם, אוֹ אֲפִלּוּ בְּחוּץ לָאָרֶץ אִם הַגֵּט מְקֻיָּם בְּחוֹתְמָיו, אוֹ לִהְיוֹת שָׁלִיחַ שֶׁל אִשָּׁה לְקַבֵּל גִּטָּהּ, לְכָל אֵלּוּ הַסּוּמָא כָּשֵׁר:


וסומא – [A blind person] is prohibited from bring a Jewish bill of divorce from outside the land of Israel, because he is unable to say, “it was written in my presence and signed in my presence.” But, to bring a Jewish bill of divorce in the land of Israel, where it is not necessary to say, “it was written in my presence and signed in my presence,” or even in the Diaspora, if the Jewish bill of divorce is verified through its signatories, or to serve as the agent of the woman to receive her Jewish bill of divorce, for all of these, the blind person is considered valid.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 2:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:5:4)


**וְנָכְרִי.** דְּלֵיתֵיהּ בְּתוֹרַת גִּטִּין וְקִדּוּשִׁין, וּבְמִידֵי דִּלְנַפְשֵׁיהּ לֹא חָזֵי לֹא מָצֵי לְמֵעֲבַד שְׁלִיחוּתָא לְאַחְרִינֵי:


ונכרי – he is not [included] in laws of Divorce and Kiddushin/betrothal for the sake of marriage, and anything of his own person is not worthy, he cannot perform the act of agency for another.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 2:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:6:1)


**קִבֵּל הַקָּטָן.** הַגֵּט מִיַּד הַבַּעַל:


קיבל הקטן – [The minor received] the Jewish bill of divorce from the hand of the husband.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 2:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:6:2)


**וְהִגְדִּיל.** קֹדֶם שֶׁמְּסָרוֹ לָהּ:


והגדיל – [became an adult] prior to his delivery of it [i.e., the Jewish bill of divorce] to her [i.e., the wife].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 2:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:6:3)


**פָּתוּחַ וְנִסְתַּמֵּא וְחָזַר וְנִתְפַּתֵּחַ.** אֲפִלּוּ לֹא חָזַר וְנִתְפַּתֵּחַ, הוֹאִיל וְהָיָה פָּתוּחַ בִּשְׁעַת קַבָּלַת הַגֵּט שַׁפִּיר מָצֵי לְמֵהָוֵי שָׁלִיחַ, דְּהָא יָכוֹל לוֹמַר בְּפָנַי נִכְתַּב וּבְפָנַי נֶחְתָּם. אֶלָּא אַיְדֵי דְּבָעֵי לְמִתְנֵי סֵיפָא וְחָזַר וְנִשְׁתַּפָּה, שֶׁצָּרִיךְ שֶׁיִּהְיֶה בֶּן דַּעַת בִּשְׁעַת נְתִינָה, תָּנָא נַמִּי רֵישָׁא וְחָזַר וְנִתְפַּתֵּחַ. וְכָל פְּסוּלֵי עֵדוּת בַּעֲבֵרָה, פְּסוּלִין לְהָבִיא אֶת הַגֵּט, שֶׁאֵין נֶאֱמָנִים לוֹמַר בְּפָנַי נִכְתַּב וּבְפָנַי נֶחְתָּם. וְאִם הַגֵּט מְקֻיָּם בְּחוֹתְמָיו, כְּשֵׁרִים לַהֲבִיאוֹ:


פתוח ונסתמא וחזר ונתפתח – even if he was not restored to being sighted, since he was able to see at the time when he received the Jewish bill of divorce, he well is able to serve as an agent for he is able to say: “it was written in my presence and it was signed in my presence.” But, since it is required for [the Mishnah] to teach the concluding segment, “and he returned to being sighted,” that he [the agent] must be cognitively aware at the time of the giving [of the Jewish bill of divorce], the Mishnah teaches also at the beginning, “and he returned to being sighted.” But all those who are invalid for testimony through sin, are also invalid to bring the Jewish bill of divorce, for they are not believed to say, “it was written in my presence and signed in my presence.” But if the Jewish bill of divorce is verified through its signatories, they are valid to bring it (i.e., the Jewish bill of divorce).



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 2:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:7:1)


**חֲמוֹתָהּ וּבַת חֲמוֹתָהּ כוּ'.** אֵינָן נֶאֱמָנוֹת לוֹמַר מֵת בַּעְלָהּ, מִשּׁוּם דְּסַנְיָן לָהּ וּמְכַוְּנִין לְקַלְקְלָהּ:


וחמותה ובת חמותה כו' – [they] are not believed to say that her husband died, because they hate her and intend to disgrace her.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 2:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/2:7:2)


**וּבִלְבַד שֶׁהִיא צְרִיכָה לוֹמַר בְּפָנַי נִכְתַּב וּבְפָנַי נֶחְתָּם.** וְדַוְקָא כְּשֶׁהִתְנָה הַבַּעַל עִמָּהּ בְּשָׁעָה שֶׁמָּסַר הַגֵּט לְיָדָהּ וְאָמַר לָהּ לֹא תִתְגָּרְשִׁי אֶלָּא בְּבֵית דִּינוֹ שֶׁל פְּלוֹנִי וְתֹאמְרִי בְּפָנַי נִכְתַּב וּבְפָנַי נֶחְתָּם. וְהַבֵּית דִּין לוֹקְחִין אוֹתוֹ מִיָּדָהּ אַחַר שֶׁאָמְרָה בְּפָנַי נִכְתַּב וּבְפָנַי נֶחְתָּם, וּמְמַנִּין שָׁלִיחַ שֶׁיַּחֲזֹר וְיִתֵּן אוֹתוֹ לָהּ. אֲבָל אִשָּׁה שֶׁגִּטָּהּ יוֹצֵא מִתַּחַת יָדָהּ בְּכָל מָקוֹם שֶׁהִיא, הֲרֵי זוֹ מְגֹרֶשֶׁת, וַאֲפִלּוּ שֶׁאֵין הַגֵּט מְקֻיָּם בְּחוֹתְמָיו, וְאֵינָהּ צְרִיכָה שֶׁתֹּאמַר בְּפָנַי נִכְתַּב וּבְפָנַי נֶחְתָּם:


ובלבד שהיא צריכה לומר בפני נכתב ובפני נחתם – and especially when the husband made a condition with her at the time that he delivered the Jewish bill of divorce to her hand and said to her: You will not be divorced other than in the court of so-and-so, and you will say, “it was written in my presence and signed in my presence.” And the court takes it [i.e., the Jewish bill of divorce] from her hand after she said, “it was written in my presence and signed in my presence,” and appoint an agent who will go back and give it [i.e., the Jewish bill of divorce] to her, but the wife whose Jewish bill of divorce leaves her hand in whatever place she happens to be, is [considered to be] divorced. And even if the Jewish bill of divorce’s signatories are not verified, and she does not have to say, “it was written in my presence and signed in my presence.”



## Chapter 3


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 3:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:1:1)


**כָּל גֵּט.** קוֹל סוֹפְרִים מַקְרִין. הַגְּדוֹלִים מַקְרִין לְתַלְמִידֵיהֶם, כְּשֶׁיָּבֹא גֵּט לְפָנֶיךָ כָּתְבֵהוּ כָּךְ. וְהִזְכִּירוּ שֵׁם אֱינַשׁ בְּעָלְמָא:


כל גט – קול סופרים מקרין – The adults read [the Jewish bill of divorce] to their students, and when the Jewish bill of divorce comes before you, write it as such, and they mentioned the name of any person.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 3:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:1:2)


**יָתֵר מִכֵּן.** וְלֹא זֶה בִּלְבַד שֶׁנִּכְתַּב שֶׁלֹּא לְשֵׁם גֵּרוּשִׁין אֶלָּא לְהִתְלַמֵּד, אֶלָּא אַף זֶה שֶׁנִּכְתַּב לְשֵׁם גֵּרוּשִׁין גְּמוּרִים וְנִמְלַךְ, פָּסוּל:


יותר מכן – and not only this – that the Jewish bill of divorce that was not written for the sake of Jewish divorce but only for practice (see Gittin 24b), but even that which was written for the sake of Jewish divorce completely, but he [i.e., the husband], changed his mind, it is invalid.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 3:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:1:3)


**יָתֵר מִכֵּן הָיוּ לוֹ שְׁתֵּי נָשִׁים כוּ'.** וְלֹא זֶה בִּלְבַד שֶׁנִּכְתַּב שֶׁלֹּא לְשֵׁם גֵּרוּשִׁין שֶׁל אָדָם זֶה, פָּסוּל, אֶלָּא אַף זֶה שֶׁהָיוּ לוֹ שְׁתֵּי נָשִׁים שֶׁנִּכְתַּב לְשֵׁם גֵּרוּשִׁין דְּהַאי גַּבְרָא, פָּסוּל, כֵּיוָן שֶׁנִּכְתַּב שֶׁלֹּא לְשֵׁם גֵּרוּשִׁין שֶׁל זוֹ:


יתר מכאן היו לו ב' נשים כו' – and not only this alone that it (i.e., the Jewish bill of divorce) was written not for the sake of divorce of this person, it is invalid. But even if he had two wives and it was written for the sake of [the] divorce of this man, it is invalid, since it was not written for the sake of divorce of that woman.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 3:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:1:4)


**גְּדוֹלָה וּקְטַנָּה.** לָאו דַוְקָא:


גדולה וקטנה – not exactly.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 3:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:1:5)


**יָתֵר מִכֵּן אָמַר לַלַּבְלָר וְכוּ'.** הָא קָא מַשְׁמַע לָן דְּלֹא אָמְרִינַן הֻבְרַר הַדָּבָר לְמַפְרֵעַ דְּבִשְׁעַת כְּתִיבָה נַמִּי דַּעְתֵּיהּ אַהָא וְאִיכָּא גֵּרוּשִׁין דִּידֵיהּ וְדִידָהּ:


יתר מכאן – it comes to teach us that we do not say that the matter is clear retroactively, for at the time it is written, it was also his intention for this one, and there is a divorce for him and her.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 3:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:2:1)


**הַכּוֹתֵב טָפְסֵי גִטִּין.** סוֹפֵר שֶׁרוֹצֶה שֶׁיִּהְיוּ מְזֻמָּנִין אֶצְלוֹ, שֶׁפְּעָמִים אָדָם בָּא לְשָׂכְרוֹ וְהוּא טָרוּד בִּשְׁטָרוֹת אֲחֵרִים:


הכותב טופסי גיטין – A scribe who wants that they should be ready at hand with him, for there are times when a person comes to hire him [to write a Jewish bill of divorce] and he is preoccupied with other documents.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 3:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:2:2)


**צָרִיךְ שֶׁיָּנִיחַ מְקוֹם הָאִישׁ וּמְקוֹם הָאִשָּׁה וּמְקוֹם הַזְּמַן.** וּבַגְּמָרָא מוֹסִיף אַף מְקוֹם הֲרֵי אַתְּ מֻתֶּרֶת לְכָל אָדָם:


צריך שיניח מקום האיש ומקום האשה ומקים הזמן – And in the Gemara, it adds even a place for [the phrase] “you are permitted to any man.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 3:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:2:3)


**מִפְּנֵי הַתַּקָּנָה.** הִתִּירוּ לִכְתֹּב טָפְסֵי גִּטִּין וּשְׁטָרוֹת שֶׁלֹּא לִשְׁמָן מִפְּנֵי תַּקָּנַת הַסּוֹפֵר שֶׁיִּהְיוּ מְזֻמָּנִים לוֹ, וּבִלְבַד שֶׁיְּשַׁיֵּר תּוֹרֵף לְכָתְבוֹ לִשְׁמָהּ. וְגָזְרִינַן תּוֹרֵף שְׁאָר שְׁטָרוֹת אַטּוּ תּוֹרֵף דְּגִטִּין:


מפני התקנה – They permitted him to write blank forms of Jewish divorce bills and other documents not for their sake, because of the scribal ordinance that they be ready at hand with him, as long as he leaves space for the part of the document that makes it binding to write it for its own sake. And we decree that the part of the document that makes it valid for writing other documents because of the part of the document that makes bills of Jewish divorce valid.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 3:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:2:4)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה פוֹסֵל בְּכֻלָּן.** דְּגָזַר טֹפֶס אַטּוּ תּוֹרֵף, וּשְׁאָר שְׁטָרוֹת אַטּוּ גִּטִּין:


רבי יהודה פוסל בכולן – For he decreed the blank forms of other documents on account of the parts of the document that make them binding, and other documents on account of Jewish bills of divorce.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 3:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:2:5)


**רַבִּי אֶלְעָזָר מַכְשִׁיר בְּכֻלָּן.** דְּלֹא גָּזַר שְׁאָר שְׁטָרוֹת אַטּוּ גִּטִּין:


ר"א מכשיר בכולן – for he did not decree other documents on account of bills of Jewish divorce.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 3:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:2:6)


**חוּץ מִגִּטֵּי נָשִׁים שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר וְכָתַב לָהּ לִשְׁמָהּ.** וְגָזְרִינַן טֹפֶס אַטּוּ תּוֹרֵף. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֶלְעָזָר:


חוץ מגטי נשים שנאמר "וכתב לה" – לשמה – And they decree that the blank forms of the document (for a Jewish bill of divorce) on account of the parts of the document that make them binding. But, the Halakha is according to Rabbi Eliezer [which are not permitted – as they must be written explicitly for the sake of the divorce and for that particular couple].



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 3:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:3:1)


**לְאַלְתַּר.** מִיָּד. כָּשֵׁר:


לאלתר – immediately, it is valid.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 3:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:3:2)


**וְאִם לָאו פָּסוּל.** וְדַוְקָא שֶׁאָבַד בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁהַשַּׁיָּרוֹת מְצוּיוֹת, דְּאִיכָּא לְמֵימַר מֵעוֹבְרִים וְשָׁבִים נָפַל. אֲבָל אִם אָבַד בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁאֵין הַשַּׁיָּרוֹת מְצוּיוֹת, אֲפִלּוּ לְאַחַר זְמַן מְרֻבֶּה כָּשֵׁר. וַאֲפִלּוּ אָבַד בְּמָקוֹם שֶׁהַשַּׁיָּרוֹת מְצוּיוֹת, אִם יֵשׁ לָעֵדִים בּוֹ סִימָן מֻבְהָק, כְּגוֹן שֶׁיֹּאמְרוּ נֶקֶב יֵשׁ בּוֹ בְּצַד אוֹת פְּלוֹנִית, אוֹ שֶׁיֹּאמְרוּ מֵעוֹלָם לֹא חָתַמְנוּ בְּגֵט בְּאֵלּוּ הַשֵּׁמוֹת אֶלָּא בָזֶה בִּלְבַד, כָּשֵׁר, וַאֲפִלּוּ לְאַחַר זְמַן מְרֻבֶּה:


ואם לאו פסול – And especially if it [the Jewish bill of divorce] was lost in a place where [regular] caravans are found, and one could say that it [the Jewish bill of divorce] fell from those who come and go. But if it was lost in a place where caravans are not [regularly] found, even after a lengthy period of time, it is valid. And even if it was lost in a place where the caravans are [regularly] found, if there [is known] to the witnesses a distinguished mark of identification, such as if they [i.e., the witnesses] say that it [i.e, the Jewish bill of divorce document] has a perforation on the side of a certain letter, or they say, that we never signed the Jewish bill of divorce with these names but rather on this one alone, it is valid, even after a lengthy period of time.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 3:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:3:3)


**בַּחֲפִיסָה אוֹ בִדְלֻסְקְמָא.** אַמְתָּחוֹת שֶׁרְגִילִין לָתֵת בָּהֶם שְׁטָרוֹת, וְיֵשׁ בָּהֶן סִימָן שֶׁהֵם שֶׁלּוֹ:


בחפיסא או בדלוסקמא – sacks in which documents are regularly placed therein and they contain a particular sign that they belong to him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 3:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:3:4)


**אִם מַכִּירוֹ כָּשֵׁר.** מִלְּתָא בְּאַנְפֵּי נַפְשָׁהּ הִיא. וְהָכִי קָאָמַר, מְצָאוֹ בַחֲפִיסָה אוֹ בִדְלֻסְקְמָא אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינוֹ מַכִּירוֹ לַגֵּט, אוֹ שֶׁמַּכִּירוֹ לַגֵּט אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁמְּצָאוֹ בְּכָל מָקוֹם, כָּשֵׁר:


אם מכירו כשר – this is a matter for itself, and this is how it should be read: If they found it in a small leather bag or in a case, even though he does not recognize it as a Jewish bill of divorce, or that he recognizes as a Jewish bill of divorce, even though he found it in any place, it is valid.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 3:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:3:5)


**נוֹתְנוֹ לָהּ בְּחֶזְקַת שֶׁהוּא קַיָּם.** וְלֹא חָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא מֵת וּבָטֵל שְׁלִיחוּתוֹ, דְּאָמְרִינַן הַעֲמֵד דָּבָר עַל חֶזְקָתוֹ. אֲבָל אִם נוֹדַע שֶׁמֵּת קֹדֶם שֶׁהִגִּיעַ גֵּט לְיָדָהּ, הַגֵּט בָּטֵל, דְּאֵין גֵּט לְאַחַר מִיתָה:


נותנו לה בחזקת שהוא קיים – And we don’t fear lest the husband died and his agency is voided, for we say, that a matter stands on its presumption. But if it is known that the husband died prior to the Jewish bill of divorce reaching her hand, the Jewish bill of divorce is voided, for there is no Jewish bill of divorce after death.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 3:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:3:6)


**מַקְרִיבִין אוֹתָהּ.** וְלֹא חָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא מֵתוּ בְּעָלֶיהָ, וְחַטָּאת שֶׁמֵּתוּ בְּעָלֶיהָ לְמִיתָה עוֹמֶדֶת:


מקריבין אותה – and we don’t fear lest its owner(s) died, and the sin offering of those whose owners died, are considered as if their death is established.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 3:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:4:1)


**כַּרְקוֹם.** תַּרְגּוּם מָצוֹר כַּרְכּוֹמִין:


כרקום – from the Aramaic translation, works of siege (the husband is presumed to be alive in such a setting as opposed to when the city is conquered).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 3:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:4:2)


**הַמִּטָּרֶפֶת.** וַעֲדַיִן לֹא טָבְעָה:


המטרפת – but has not yet sunk.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 3:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:4:3)


**לִדּוֹן.** בְּדִינֵי נְפָשׁוֹת:


לידון – in [a matter of] capital crimes.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 3:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:4:4)


**בַּת יִשְׂרָאֵל לְכֹהֵן.** חֻמְרֵי מֵתִים:


בת ישראל לכהן – the stringencies regarding death


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 3:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:4:5)


**בַּת כֹּהֵן לְיִשְׂרָאֵל.** חֻמְרֵי חַיִּים:


בת כהן לישראל – the stringencies regarding life



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 3:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:5:1)


**הַמֵּבִיא גֵט בְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל.** שֶׁאֵין צָרִיךְ לוֹמַר בְּפָנַי נִכְתַּב וּבְפָנַי נֶחְתָּם:


המביא גט בארץ ישראל – where it is not necessary to say, “it was written in my presence and signed in my presence.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 3:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:5:2)


**מְשַׁלְּחוֹ בְיַד אַחֵר.** וְעוֹשֶׂה שָׁלִיחַ מֵאֵלָיו וְלֹא בְּבֵית דִּין. וְדַוְקָא חָלָה:


משלחו ביד אחר – And makes him [of the other] an agent on his own and not in the Jewish court, and specifically if he had become ill.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 3:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:5:3)


**וְאִם אָמַר לוֹ.** בַּעַל לַשָּׁלִיחַ:


ואם אמר לו – the husband to the agent


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 3:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:5:4)


**טֹל לִי הֵימֶנָּה חֵפֶץ פְּלוֹנִי.** כְּשֶׁתִּתֵּן לָהּ אֶת הַגֵּט. לֹא יְשַׁלְּחֵנוּ בְּיַד אַחֵר:


טול לי הימנה חפץ פלוני – When you give her the Jewish bill of divorce



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 3:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:6:1)


**עוֹשֶׂה בֵית דִּין.** שָׁלִיחַ, וּמְשַׁלְּחוֹ:


עושה בית דין – [the court appoints] an agent and sends him


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 3:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:6:2)


**שְׁלִיחַ בֵּית דִּין אָנִי.** וּמִסְתָּמָא בֵּית דִּין עָשׂוּ הַדָּבָר בְּהֶכְשֵׁרוֹ. וְשָׁלִיחַ שֵׁנִי מְמַנֶּה שָׁלִיחַ שְׁלִישִׁי עַד מֵאָה, וְכֻלָּן בְּבֵית דִּין, מִדִּתְנַן בְּמַתְנִיתִין אֵין הַשָּׁלִיחַ הָאַחֲרוֹן כוּ'‏ אֶלָּא אוֹמֵר שְׁלִיחַ בֵּית דִּין אָנִי:


שליח בית דין אני – without further qualification, the Jewish court did this validating him, and the second agent appoints a third agent, [and so-on-and-so-forth] until [the] one-hundred[th agent], and all of them are [appointed] in the Jewish court, since it is taught in our Mishnah, “the last agent [does not have to say, ‘it was written in my presence and signed in my presence’]” but only says that “I am the agent of the [Jewish] court.”



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 3:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:7:1)


**לִהְיוֹת מַפְרִישׁ עֲלֵיהֶן מֵחֶלְקָן.** כְּשֶׁיַּפְרִישׁ הַתְּרוּמָה יִמְכְּרֶנָּהּ וִיעַכֵּב הַדָּמִים לְעַצְמוֹ בִּשְׁבִיל חוֹבוֹ שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ עַל הַכֹּהֵן. וּמַעֲשֵׂר רִאשׁוֹן וּמַעֲשַׂר עָנִי יְעַכֵּב וְיֹאכַל בִּשְׁבִיל הַחוֹב שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ עַל הַלֵּוִי וְעַל הֶעָנִי, אֶלָּא שֶׁמַּפְרִישׁ מִמַּעֲשֵׂר רִאשׁוֹן תְּרוּמַת מַעֲשֵׂר לַכֹּהֵן. וְאִם הוּא רָגִיל לִתֵּן תְּרוּמוֹתָיו וּמַעַשְׂרוֹתָיו לָזֶה הַכֹּהֵן אוֹ הַלֵּוִי אוֹ הֶעָנִי שֶׁהִלְוָה לָהֶן, אֵין צָרִיךְ לְזַכּוֹת לָהֶם מַעַשְׂרוֹתָיו וּתְרוּמוֹתָיו עַל יַד אַחֵר, אֶלָּא לוֹקְחָן לְעַצְמוֹ מִיַּד אַחַר שֶׁיַּפְרִישֵׁם. אֲבָל אִם רָגִיל לִתֵּן תְּרוּמוֹתָיו וּמַעַשְׂרוֹתָיו לַאֲחֵרִים, אֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לְעַכְּבָן בְּחוֹבוֹ עַד שֶׁיְּזַכֶּה לָהֶם עַל יְדֵי אַחֵר תְּחִלָּה, וְאַחַר כָּךְ יַחֲזֹר וְיִטְּלֵם בְּחוֹבוֹ:


להיות מפריש עליהן מחלקן – When he separates out the Terumah/heave-offering (which goes to the Kohen) , he should [first] sell it, he should detain the money to himself on account of his own obligation that he has towards the Kohen/priest. And the first tithe and second tithe he should withhold [them] and eat it for the obligation that he has towards the Levite and the poor, but he (the Levite) separates out from the first tithe [that he has received] the heave-offering (of one-tenth) [that he is required to give to the Kohen/priest]. But if he is accustomed to give his heave-offerings and tithes to this particular Kohen or particular Levite or the poor person who lent to them, he does not need to transfer to them their tithes and heave-offerings through another person, but he takes them for himself immediately after he has separated them out. But if he was accustomed to give his heave-offerings and tithes to others, he may not withhold them through his obligation, until he takes possession of them through another [Kohen or Levite] first and thereafter he should go back and take them as part of his obligatory [gift].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 3:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:7:2)


**צָרִיךְ לִטֹּל רְשׁוּת מִן הַיּוֹרְשִׁים.** שֶׁיָּרְשׁוּ מֵהֶם קַרְקַע מְשֻׁעְבֶּדֶת לְבַעַל חוֹב, צָרִיךְ לִטֹּל מֵהֶם רְשׁוּת אִם רוֹצִים לִפְרֹעַ חוֹב זֶה מִתְּרוּמוֹת וּמַעַשְׂרוֹת הַלָּלוּ, דְּשֶׁמָּא רוֹצִים הֵם לִקַּח מַתְּנוֹתֵיהֶן וְלִפְרֹעַ חוֹב מוֹרִישָׁם מִמָּקוֹם אַחֵר:


צריך ליטול רשות מן היורשים – That inherited from them property that had been mortgaged to a creditor, he must take permission from them if they want to repay this loan through these heave-offerings and tithes [that they have received], lest they wish to take their [priestly/Levitical] gifts and to repay their hereditary loans from another place.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 3:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:8:1)


**הַמַּנִּיחַ פֵּרוֹת לִהְיוֹת מַפְרִישׁ עֲלֵיהֶן כוּ'.** סוֹמֵךְ עַל אֵלּוּ וְאוֹכֵל טְבָלִים אֲחֵרִים שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ, וְאוֹמֵר, הֲרֵי תְרוּמָתָן בְּאוֹתָן פֵּרוֹת שֶׁהִקְצֵיתִי לְכָךְ:


המניח פירות להיות מפריש עליהן כו' – He relies upon these and eat from other TEVEL/eatables forbidden to be consumed prior to the separation of sacred gifts that he has, and says, ‘Behold their heave-offerings [come] from these fruits that I have set aside for this purpose.’


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 3:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:8:2)


**וְאִם אָבְדוּ.** הָלַךְ לְבָדְקָן וּמְצָאָן שֶׁאָבְדוּ:


ואם אבדו – He went to check them and found that they had become lost.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 3:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:8:3)


**הֲרֵי זֶה חוֹשֵׁשׁ.** לְאוֹתָן טְבָלִים שֶׁתִּקֵּן בְּהַבְטָחָתָן שֶׁל אֵלּוּ. וְאִם לֹא אֲכָלָן צָרִיךְ לְהַפְרִישׁ מֵהֶם, שֶׁמָּא כְּשֶׁאָמַר הֲרֵי תְרוּמָתָן בַּפֵּרוֹת שֶׁהִקְצֵיתִי כְּבָר הָיוּ אֲבוּדִין:


הרי זה חושש – for these selfsame eatables forbidden to be consumed prior to the separation of sacred gifts, which he had legally fit for use by giving the priestly dues with the promise of those. And if he did not eat them, he must separate [tithes] from them, lest when he says: ‘Behold their priest-due is with the fruit that I have set aside’ have already become lost.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 3:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:8:4)


**מֵעֵת לְעֵת.** שֶׁל בְּדִיקָה. כְּשֶׁבְּדָקָן וּמְצָאָן אֲבוּדִין חוֹשֵׁשׁ שֶׁמָּא מֵאֶתְמוֹל בָּעֵת הַזֹּאת אָבְדוּ, וְאִם עֲשָׂאָן מַעֲשֵׂר תּוֹךְ מֵעֵת לְעֵת עַל פֵּרוֹת אֲחֵרִים צָרִיךְ לְהַפְרִישׁ עֲלֵיהֶם מִסָּפֵק. וּטְפֵי מֵהָכִי לֹא אַחְמוּר רַבָּנָן לְמֵיחַשׁ, אֶלָּא סוֹמְכִין אַחֲזָקָה:


מעת לעת – of being examined. And when he checked them and found them to be lost, he fears lest from yesterday at that particular time they had been lost, and if he had designed them as tithes in the midst of the time period of twenty-four astronomical hours over other fruit. He must separate from them [tithes] out of doubt, and more than this, the Rabbis were not stringent to fear, but they rely upon the presumptive continuance of an actual condition until evidence of change is produced.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 3:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:8:5)


**בּוֹדְקִין אֶת הַיַּיִן.** שֶׁהִנִּיחוֹ לִהְיוֹת מַפְרִישׁ עָלָיו, צָרִיךְ לְבָדְקוֹ שֶׁמָּא הֶחְמִיץ, וְאֵין תּוֹרְמִין מִן הַחֹמֶץ עַל הַיַּיִן:


בודקין את היין – that he left it to [have tithes] separated out, he must check it, lest it soured, since we don’t offer heave-offerings from wine that had soured.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 3:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:8:6)


**בְּקָדִים שֶׁל מוֹצָאֵי הֶחָג.** כְּשֶׁמְּנַשֶּׁבֶת רוּחַ קָדִים בְּמוֹצָאֵי הֶחָג:


בקדים של מוצאי החג – since when the east wind blows at the conclusion of the festival [of Sukkot].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 3:8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/3:8:7)


**וּבִשְׁעַת כְּנִיסַת מַיִם.** כְּשֶׁהֵן כְּפוֹל הַלָּבָן נִקְרָאִים בֹּסֶר. וּכְשֶׁהַלַּחְלוּחִית נִכְנֶסֶת וּגְדֵלָה בְּתוֹכוֹ שֶׁיָּכוֹל לַעֲצֹר מֵהֶן כָּל שֶׁהוּא, הַיְנוּ כְּנִיסַת מַיִם. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר, הָיוּ כּוֹתְשִׁים הָעֲנָבִים כְּשֶׁהֵן בֹּסֶר וְנוֹתְנִים לְתוֹכוֹ מַיִם וְעוֹשִׂים חֹמֶץ לְטַבֵּל. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


בשעת כניסת המים – when they are like the white bean (see Mishnah Kilayim 1:1), they are called half-ripe fruit (i.e. grapes). But when the moistness enters and grows in it when one is able to store away from them a bit, that is at the time when the water enters. Another explanation: When they were crushing the grapes when they were half-ripe, and putting water into it, and making vinegar to make it subject to setting aside sacred gifts. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehuda.



## Chapter 4


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:1:1)


**הַשּׁוֹלֵחַ וְהִגִּיעַ בַּשָּׁלִיחַ.** שֶׁלֹּא הָיָה מִתְכַּוֵּן לִרְדֹּף אַחֲרָיו לְהַשִּׂיגוֹ, אֶלָּא שֶׁנִּשְׁתַּהָה הַשָּׁלִיחַ בַּדֶּרֶךְ וְהָיָה לוֹ לָזֶה דֶּרֶךְ לְשָׁם וְרָאָהוּ וּבִטֵּל הַגֵּט, אֲפִלּוּ הָכִי בָּטֵל, וְלֹא אָמְרִינַן לִצְעוּרָהּ בְּעָלְמָא מְכַוֵּן, שֶׁאִם הָיָה בְדַעְתּוֹ לְבַטְּלוֹ הָיָה רוֹדֵף אַחֲרָיו:


השולח גט והגיע בשליח – for he had not intended to pursue after him to overtake him, but rather, when the agent got detained along the way and he [the husband] was on the path to there and saw him and nullified the Jewish bill of divorce, even so, it is nullified. And we don’t say that he intended to merely cause suffering, for if he had intended to nullify it, he (the husband) would have pursued after him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:1:2)


**אֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לְבַטְּלוֹ.** הָא קָא מַשְׁמַע לָן דְּאַף עַל פִּי דְּחָזִינַן לֵיהּ דְּמַהֲדַר עֲלֵיהּ מֵעִקָּרָא לְבִטּוּלֵיהּ, לֹא אָמְרִינַן אִגַּלָּאִי מִלְּתָא לְמַפְרֵעַ דְּבַטּוֹלֵי בַטְּלֵיהּ. וְהֵיכָא דְיָהִיב אֱינַשׁ גִּטָּא לִדְבִיתְהוּ לִזְמַן אוֹ עַל תְּנַאי, אִי אָמַר לָהּ הֲרֵי זֶה גִטֵּךְ מֵעַכְשָׁיו וְלִזְמַן פְּלוֹנִי אוֹ אִם יִתְקַיֵּם תְּנַאי פְּלוֹנִי, מִכִּי מָטֵי גִּטָּהּ לְיָדָהּ לֹא מָצֵי תוּ לְבַטְלֵיהּ, וְתִהְיֶה מְגֹרֶשֶׁת לְאוֹתוֹ זְמַן אוֹ כְּשֶׁיִּתְקַיֵּם הַתְּנַאי. וְאִי לֹא אָמַר לָהּ מֵעַכְשָׁיו, אֲפִלּוּ לְבָתַר דִּמְטָא גִּטָּהּ לְיָדָהּ מָצֵי מְבַטֵּל לֵיהּ:


אינו יכול לבטלו – that comes to teach us that even though we saw him that he ran after him to nullify it, we don’t say that this matter was revealed retroactively that he (i.e., the husband) had revoked the Jewish bill of divorce. For where a person gives a Jewish bill of divorce to his wife for a time or on condition, or says to her, “Behold this is your Jewish bill of divorce from now or from this [particular] time”; or if the specific condition had been fulfilled, and if he did not say to her “from now” – even if after her Jewish bill of divorce had reached her hand, he can annul it.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:2:1)


**בָּרִאשׁוֹנָה.** לֹא הָיָה מְבַטְּלוֹ בִּפְנֵי הָאִשָּׁה וְלֹא בִּפְנֵי הַשָּׁלִיחַ, אֶלָּא בַּמָּקוֹם שֶׁהָיָה עוֹמֵד הָיָה מְבַטְּלוֹ בִּפְנֵי שְׁלֹשָׁה:


בראשונה – He would not nullify it in the presence of the woman nor in the presence of the agent, but in the place where he is standing, he would nullify it in the presence of three [judges].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:2:2)


**מִפְּנֵי תִקּוּן הָעוֹלָם.** שֶׁהַשָּׁלִיחַ שֶׁאֵינוֹ יוֹדֵעַ בַּדָּבָר מוֹלִיכוֹ לָהּ וְהִיא נִשֵּׂאת בּוֹ. וּמִכֹּחַ תַּקָּנַת רַבָּן גַּמְלִיאֵל מַלְקִין עַל מִי שֶׁמְּבַטֵּל הַגֵּט אוֹ מוֹסֵר מוֹדָעָא עַל הַגֵּט:


מפני תקון העולם – that the agent who does not know of the matter would bring it to her and she would [as a result] marry [again] through it. And on the strength of the ordinance of Rabban Gamaliel, we flog whomever nullifies the Jewish bill of divorce [in a Jewish court in another place] or issues a protest/ declaration before witnesses against a forced or unduly influenced action concerning the Jewish bill of divorce.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:2:3)


**בָּרִאשׁוֹנָה הָיָה מְשַׁנֶּה שְׁמוֹ וּשְׁמָהּ.** כְּשֶׁהָיוּ לוֹ שְׁנֵי שֵׁמוֹת, אֶחָד כָּאן וְאֶחָד בִּמְדִינַת הַיָּם, הָיָה מְגָרְשָׁהּ בַּשֵּׁם הַנּוֹהֵג בִּמְקוֹם כְּתִיבַת הַגֵּט, וְלֹא הָיָה מַקְפִּיד לִכְתֹּב אֶת שְׁנֵיהֶם:


בראשונה היה משנה שמו ושמה – when he (i.e. the husband) had two names – one here (in the land of Israel) and another abroad, he would divorce her with the name common used in the place where the Jewish bill of divorce was written, and he would not be exacting to write both [names].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:2:4)


**מִפְּנֵי תִקּוּן הָעוֹלָם.** שֶׁלֹּא יוֹצִיאוּ לַעַז עַל בָּנֶיהָ מִן הַשֵּׁנִי, לוֹמַר לֹא גֵּרְשָׁהּ בַּעְלָהּ שֶׁאֵין זֶה שְׁמוֹ. וְאָדָם שֶׁהֻחְזַק בִּשְׁנֵי שֵׁמוֹת בִּשְׁנֵי מְקוֹמוֹת, חַד בִּמְקוֹם הַכְּתִיבָה וְחַד בִּמְקוֹם הַנְּתִינָה, אֵינָהּ מְגֹרֶשֶׁת עַד שֶׁיִּכְתֹּב שֵׁם שֶׁל מְקוֹם הַנְּתִינָה וְשֵׁם שֶׁל מְקוֹם הַכְּתִיבָה עִמּוֹ. אֲבָל אִם הֻחְזַק בִּשְׁנֵי שֵׁמוֹת בְּמָקוֹם אֶחָד וְכָתַב אֶחָד מִן הַשֵּׁמוֹת, בְּדִיעֲבַד הַגֵּט כָּשֵׁר. מִיהוּ לְכַתְּחִלָּה צָרִיךְ לִכְתֹּב שְׁנֵיהֶם. וְהֵיכָא דְּשִׁנָּה שְׁמוֹ אוֹ שְׁמָהּ בַּגֵּט, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁכָּתַב אַחַר כָּךְ וְכָל שֵׁם שֶׁיֵּשׁ לִי, הַגֵּט בָּטֵל:


מפני תיקון העולם – so as to not cast aspersion on [the legitimacy of] her children from the second [name of her] husband, to say, that her husband didn’t divorce her for that is not his name. And a person who is known by two names in two [different] places, one in the place where [the Jewish bill of divorce] was written and another in the place where it is delivered/given, she is not divorced until the name of the place where [the Jewish bill of divorce] is delivered/given, and the name of the place where it was written with it. But, if he was known by two names in one place and he wrote [in the Jewish bill of divorce] (only) one of those names, post facto, the Jewish bill of divorce is valid. However, ab initio, one must write both of [the names] and where he changed his name or her name in the Jewish bill of divorce, even though he wrote after it, “and every name that he has,” the Jewish bill of divorce is null and void.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:3:1)


**אֵין אַלְמָנָה נִפְרַעַת.** כְּתֻבָּתָהּ:


אין אלמנה נפרעת – Her Ketubah/Jewish marriage contract


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:3:2)


**מִנִּכְסֵי יְתוֹמִים, אֶלָּא בִשְׁבוּעָה.** שֶׁלֹּא נִתְקַבְּלָה כְּלוּם:


מנכסי יתומים, אלא בשבועה – she had not been made the recipient of anything


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:3:3)


**נִמְנְעוּ מִלְּהַשְׁבִּיעָהּ.** שֶׁמִּפְּנֵי שֶׁהִיא טוֹרַחַת לִפְנֵי הַיְּתוֹמִים מוֹרָה הֶתֵּרָא לְעַצְמָהּ לִשָּׁבַע שֶׁלֹּא לָקְחָה כְּלוּם. וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלָּקְחָה דָּבָר מֻעָט סְבוּרָה שֶׁבִּשְׂכַר טִרְחָה נוֹטַלְתּוֹ וְאֵינוֹ מִפִּרְעוֹן כְּתֻבָּתָהּ. וּלְפִיכָךְ נִמְנְעוּ מִלְּהַשְׁבִּיעָהּ וְהָיְתָה מַפְסֶדֶת כְּתֻבָּתָהּ:


נמנעו מלהשביע – because she is preoccupied before the orphans, she provides herself with a legal permission to take an oath that she had not taken anything. But even though she took a small amount, she believes that she is taking it as payment for her trouble [involved], but it is not for the paying off of the obligations [owed her] from her Jewish marriage contract.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:3:4)


**הִתְקִין רַבָּן גַּמְלִיאֵל שֶׁתְּהֵא נוֹדֶרֶת לַיְתוֹמִים כָּל מַה שֶּׁיִּרְצוּ.** כְּגוֹן קוֹנָם מִינֵי מְזוֹנוֹת עָלַי אִם נֶהֱנֵיתִי מִכְּתֻבָּתִי:


התקין רבן גמליאל שתהא נודרת ליתומים כל מה שירצו – such as קונם/taking upon myself a vow of abstinence from kinds of food if I have derived benefit from my Jewish marriage contract.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:3:5)


**וְגוֹבָה כְתֻבָּתָהּ.** וְאִם נִשֵּׂאת לְאַחֵר קֹדֶם שֶׁהִדִּירוּהָ הַיְתוֹמִים עַל כְּתֻבָּתָהּ, וְאִם יַדִּירוּהָ אַחַר שֶׁנִּשֵּׂאת, שֶׁמָּא יָפֵר לָהּ בַּעְלָהּ, כֵּיצַד יַעֲשׂוּ, מַשְׁבִּיעִין אוֹתָהּ חוּץ לְבֵית דִּין שְׁבוּעָה דְּרַבָּנָן שֶׁאֵין עָנְשָׁהּ מְרֻבֶּה, וְנוֹטֶלֶת כְּתֻבָּתָהּ אַחַר שֶׁנִּשֵּׂאת. וּכְשֶׁבָּאת לִגְבּוֹת כְּתֻבָּתָהּ קֹדֶם שֶׁתִּנָּשֵׂא לְאַחֵר, הָרְשׁוּת בְּיַד הַיְתוֹמִים, רָצוּ מַשְׁבִּיעִין אוֹתָהּ חוּץ לְבֵית דִּין, אוֹ מַדִּירִים אוֹתָהּ בְּבֵית דִּין:


וגובה כתובתה – but if she married to another [man] prior to being made by the orphans to take a vow, and if they made her take a vow after she got married, lest her husband makes her take a vow, How should do this? They make her take an oath imposed by the Rabbis outside of the court for which her punishment would not be great, and she collects her Ketubah/Jewish marriage contract after she has married. But, if she comes to collect [the value of] her Ketubah before she marries another [man], it is up to the orphans [as to what to do]: if they want, they have her take an oath outside of the court or make her take a vow in court.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:3:6)


**הָעֵדִים חוֹתְמִים עַל הַגֵּט מִפְּנֵי תִקּוּן הָעוֹלָם.** אַתַּרְוַיְהוּ קָאֵי, גַּבֵּי אַלְמָנָה שֶׁתְּהֵא נוֹדֶרֶת לַיּוֹרְשִׁים כָּל מַה שֶּׁיִּרְצוּ, מִפְּנֵי תִקּוּן הָעוֹלָם, שֶׁיִּהְיוּ הַנָּשִׁים נִשָּׂאוֹת לְבַעֲלֵיהֶן וְלֹא תִּדְאַגְנָה לְהַפְסִיד כְּתֻבָּתָן. וְגַבֵּי עֵדִים חוֹתְמִים עַל הַגֵּט מִפְּנֵי תִקּוּן הָעוֹלָם, דְּהוֹאִיל וְעֵדֵי מְסִירָה כָּרְתֵי, שֶׁהָעֵדִים שֶׁנִּמְסַר הַגֵּט בִּפְנֵיהֶם לָאִשָּׁה הֵם עִקַּר הַגֵּרוּשִׁין, לֹא הָיָה צָרִיךְ שֶׁיַּחְתְּמוּ עֵדִים בַּגֵּט, אֶלָּא מִפְּנֵי תִקּוּן הָעוֹלָם, דְּחַיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא יָמוּת אֶחָד מִן הָעֵדִים שֶׁנִּמְסַר הַגֵּט בִּפְנֵיהֶם, וְנִמְצָא הַגֵּט שֶׁבְּיָדָהּ בְּלֹא עֵדִים כְּחַסְפָּא בְּעָלְמָא:


העדים חותמים על הגט מפני תיקון העולם – It refers to both [the witnesses signing on the Jewish bill of divorce as well as the widow taking a vow before the orphans before collecting the value of her Jewish marriage contract], concerning a widow who takes a vow to the orphans on whatever they want, for the sake of the social order, so that women can marry their husbands without worrying about losing the value of their Ketubah. But concerning the witnesses who sign the Jewish bill of divorce for the sake of the social order, for since it is the witnesses to the delivery [of the Jewish bill of divorce] that effect the divorce, for the witnesses in whose presence the Jewish bill of divorce is delivered to the woman are the essential [aspect] of the divorce. It would not have been necessary for witnesses to sign the Jewish bill of divorce, other than for the sake of the social order, for we are concerned lest one of the witnesses before whom the Jewish bill of divorce was delivered would die, and that the bill of divorce in her hand without witnesses would be like a mere potsherd.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:3:7)


**הִלֵּל הִתְקִין פְּרוֹזְבּוּל.** שֶׁרָאָה אֶת הָעָם שֶׁנִּמְנְעוּ מִלְּהַלְווֹת זֶה אֶת זֶה וְעָבְרוּ עַל מַה שֶּׁכָּתוּב בַּתּוֹרָה (דברים טו) הִשָּׁמֶר לְךָ פֶּן יִהְיֶה דָּבָר עִם לְבָבְךָ בְּלִיַּעַל, עָמַד וְהִתְקִין פְּרוֹזְבּוּל. וְזֶה גּוּפוֹ שֶׁל פְּרוֹזְבּוּל, מוֹסֵר אֲנִי לָכֶם פְּלוֹנִי וּפְלוֹנִי דַּיָּנִים, שֶׁכָּל חוֹב שֶׁיֵּשׁ לִי אֵצֶל פְּלוֹנִי, שֶׁאֶגְבֶּנּוּ כָּל זְמַן שֶׁאֶרְצֶה:


הלל התקין פרוזבול – for he [Hillel] saw that the people were prevented from making loans to one another and they violated what is written in the Torah (Deuteronomy 15:9): “Beware lest you harbor the base thought, [the seventh year, the year of remission is approaching…’],” he [Hillel] ordained the Prozbul. And this is the body of the Prozbul: “I hand over to you, so-and-so and so-and-so, the judges that every debt obligation that I have with so-and-so, I will collect it whenever I want.”



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:4:1)


**עֶבֶד שֶׁנִּשְׁבָּה וּפְדָאוּהוּ.** יִשְׂרָאֵלִים אֲחֵרִים לְאַחַר שֶׁנִּתְיָאֵשׁ רַבּוֹ מִמֶּנּוּ:


עבד שנשבה ופדאוהו – Other Israelites [redeemed him] after his master had despaired of recovering him [of getting him back from captivity].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:4:2)


**אִם לְשׁוּם עֶבֶד יִשְׁתַּעְבֵּד.** לְרַבּוֹ שֵׁנִי:


אם לשום עבד ישתעבד – to his second master.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:4:3)


**לְשׁוּם בֶּן חוֹרִין לֹא יִשְׁתַּעְבֵּד.** לֹא לְרַבּוֹ רִאשׁוֹן, וְלֹא לְרַבּוֹ שֵׁנִי. לְרַבּוֹ שֵׁנִי לֹא, דְּהָא לְשׁוּם בֶּן חוֹרִין פַּרְקֵיהּ. לְרַבּוֹ רִאשׁוֹן לֹא, דִּלְמָא מִמַּנְעִי. וְלֹא פָּרְקֵי:


לשום בן חורין לא ישתעבד – No to his first master nor to his second master. Not to his second master, that behold for the sake of become a free person, he would be redeemed; not to his first master either so that everyone would subject himself to the army and remove himself from his master.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:4:4)


**רַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל אוֹמֵר בֵּין כָּךְ וּבֵין כָּךְ יִשְׁתַּעְבֵּד.** לְרַבּוֹ רִאשׁוֹן. שֶׁלֹּא יְהֵא כָּל אֶחָד וְאֶחָד הוֹלֵךְ וּמַפִּיל עַצְמוֹ לִגְיָסוֹת וּמַפְקִיעַ עַצְמוֹ מִיַּד רַבּוֹ:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:4:5)


**אַפּוֹתִיקִי.** פֹּה תְהֵא קָאֵי. מִזֶּה תִּגְבֶּה חוֹבְךָ וְלֹא מִמָּקוֹם אַחֵר:


אפותיקי – Here you would stand. From this, you will collect your lien and not from another place.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:4:6)


**וְשִׁחְרְרוֹ.** רַבּוֹ רִאשׁוֹן:


ושחררו – his first master


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:4:7)


**שׁוּרַת הַדִּין אֵין הָעֶבֶד חַיָּב כְּלוּם.** לְרַבּוֹ שֵׁנִי. שֶׁהַשִּׁחְרוּר שֶׁשִּׁחְרְרוֹ רַבּוֹ רִאשׁוֹן מַפְקִיעוֹ מִידֵי שִׁעְבּוּד:


שורת הדין אין העבד חייב כלום – to his second master, for the manumission that the first master freed him removes him from enslavement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:4:8)


**אֶלָּא מִפְּנֵי תִקּוּן הָעוֹלָם.** שֶׁמָּא יִמְצְאֶנּוּ רַבּוֹ שֵׁנִי בַּשּׁוּק וְיֹאמַר לוֹ עַבְדִּי אַתָּה וְיוֹצִיא לַעַז עַל בָּנָיו:


אלא מפני תקון העולם – lest his second master would find him in the market and say to him: “you are my servant and cast aspersion on the purity of his children.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:4:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:4:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:4:9)


**כּוֹפִין אֶת רַבּוֹ.** שֵׁנִי, וְעוֹשֵׂהוּ בֶּן חוֹרִין. וְכוֹתֵב לוֹ הָעֶבֶד שְׁטַר חוֹב עַל דָּמָיו, כְּלוֹמַר כְּפִי מַה שֶּׁהוּא שָׁוֶה לִמָּכֵר בַּשּׁוּק, לֹא כְּפִי הַחוֹב אִם הָיָה הַחוֹב יָתֵר עַל דָּמָיו:


כופין את רבו – The second [master], and make him (i.e., the slave) a free man. And the slave should write a document of liability regarding his worth, that is to say, according to what he would be worth to be sold in the marketplace, not according to the liability if the liability was greater than his monetary worth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:4:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:4:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:4:10)


**רַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל אוֹמֵר אֵין הָעֶבֶד כּוֹתֵב.** שְׁטַר חוֹב, שֶׁהוּא אֵינוֹ חַיָּב כְּלוּם. אֶלָּא רַבּוֹ רִאשׁוֹן שֶׁהִזִּיק שִׁעְבּוּדוֹ שֶׁל זֶה, הוּא שֶׁצָּרִיךְ לְשַׁלֵּם לוֹ דָמָיו, שֶׁהַמַּזִּיק שִׁעְבּוּדוֹ שֶׁל חֲבֵרוֹ חַיָּב. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל:
