רשב"ג אמר אין העבד כותב – [the slave does not write] a document of liability, for he is not liable for anything., but the first master who damaged his servitude, he is the one who must pay his worth, for the one who damages the servitude of his fellow is liable and the Halakha is according to Rabban Shimon ben Gamaliel.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:5:1)


**מִי שֶׁחֶצְיוֹ עֶבֶד וְחֶצְיוֹ בֶן חוֹרִין.** כְּגוֹן עֶבֶד שֶׁל שְׁנֵי שֻׁתָּפִים וְשִׁחְרְרוֹ אֶחָד מֵהֶם. אִי נַמִּי שֶׁקִּבֵּל רַבּוֹ מִמֶּנּוּ חֲצִי דָּמָיו וְשִׁחְרֵר חֶצְיוֹ בְּאוֹתָם הַדָּמִים:


מי שחציו עבד וחציו בן חורין – such as a slave of two partners, who was freed by one of them. Alternatively, his master received from him half of his financial worth, and freed that half on account of those funds.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:5:2)


**תִּקַּנְתֶּם אֶת רַבּוֹ.** שֶׁאֵינוֹ חָסֵר כְּלוּם:


תקנתם את רבו – who is not lacking for anything


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:5:3)


**לִשָּׂא שִׁפְחָה אֵינוֹ יָכוֹל.** מִפְּנֵי צַד חֵרוּת שֶׁבּוֹ:


לישא שפחה אינו יכול – because of the side of “freedom” within him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:5:4)


**בַּת חוֹרִין אֵינוֹ יָכוֹל.** מִפְּנֵי צַד עַבְדוּת שֶׁבּוֹ:


בת חורין אינו יכול – because of the side of enslavement within him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:5:5)


**כּוֹפִין אֶת רַבּוֹ וְעוֹשֵׂהוּ בֶן חוֹרִין.** וְהוּא הַדִּין אִם הָיָה עֶבֶד שֶׁל מֵאָה שֻׁתָּפִין וְאֶחָד מֵהֶם שִׁחְרְרוֹ, שֶׁכּוֹפִין אֶת כֻּלָּם לְשַׁחְרְרוֹ:


כופין את רבו ועושהו בן חורין – And the same law applies if he was the slave of one-hundred partners and one of them freed him; we force all of them to free him.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:6:1)


**יָצָא בֶן חוֹרִין.** אִם בָּרַח מִן הַנָּכְרִי, אוֹ שֶׁקָּנְסוּ אוֹתוֹ בֵּית דִּין לִפְדּוֹתוֹ מִן הַנָּכְרִים, כִּדְאָמְרִינַן כּוֹפִין אוֹתוֹ לִפְדּוֹתוֹ. וְאַחַר שֶׁפְּדָאוֹ לֹא יִשְׁתַּעְבֵּד בּוֹ. וּקְנַס חֲכָמִים הוּא, לְפִי שֶׁהִפְקִיעוֹ מִן הַמִּצְוֹת. וְכֵן לְחוּצָה לָאָרֶץ יָצָא לְחֵרוּת, לְפִי שֶׁהוֹצִיאוֹ מֵאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל:


בן חורין – if he fled from the heathen, or the or the court fined him [i.e., the original owner] to redeem him from the heathens, as we said – we force him to redeem him and after he has redeemed him, he does not free him, and this is a fine of the Sages, because he releases him from the commandments. And similarly, if he [i.e., the master], sold him outside of the Land [of Israel], he [i.e., the slave] goes free, since he took him outside of the Land of Israel.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:6:2)


**מִפְּנֵי תִקּוּן הָעוֹלָם.** שֶׁלֹּא יִמְסְרוּ הַנָּכְרִים עַצְמָן לְהַרְבּוֹת לְהָבִיא שְׁבוּיִין כְּשֶׁרוֹאִין שֶׁמּוֹכְרִין אוֹתָם בְּיוֹתֵר מִכְּדֵי דְּמֵיהֶם:


מפני תקון העולם – they would not surrender the heathens themselves [to the authorities] to increase and bring captives when we see that they are selling them for more than their worth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:6:3)


**וְאֵין מַבְרִיחִין אֶת הַשְּׁבוּיִין מִפְּנֵי תִקּוּן הָעוֹלָם.** שֶׁמָּא יִקְצְפוּ עַל הַשְּׁבוּיִין הָעֲתִידִים לָבֹא לְיָדָם וְיִתְּנוּם בְּשַׁלְשְׁלָאוֹת וְיָשִׂימוּ בַּסַּד רַגְלָם:


אין מבריחין את השבויין מפני תקון העולם – lest they arouse to anger the captives who will eventually come into their hands and place them in chains and put them on the splints of his legs.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:6:4)


**רַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל אוֹמֵר מִפְּנֵי תַקָּנַת הַשְּׁבוּיִין.** דְּלֹא חָיֵישׁ רַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל עַל הָעֲתִידִים לָבֹא בַּשִּׁבְיָה, אֶלָּא עַל אוֹתָם שֶׁהֵם שְׁבוּיִים עַתָּה עִמּוֹ. שֶׁאִם אֵין שָׁבוּי אֶלָּא הוּא יְחִידִי, מַבְרִיחִין אוֹתוֹ, וְאֵין לָחוּשׁ כָּאן מִפְּנֵי תַקָּנַת שְׁבוּיִים אֲחֵרִים כְּשֶׁאֵין שְׁבוּיִים אֲחֵרִים עִמּוֹ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל:


רבן שמעון בן גמליאל אומר מפני תקנת השבויין – Rabban Shimon ben Gamaliel has not been concerned about the future as a captive, other than with those who are currently in captivity with him. If there is no other captive than him alone, we force him [to free him] and we are not concerned here about the decree of other captives when there are no others with him. And the Halakha is according to Rabban Shimon ben Gamaliel.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:7:1)


**מִשּׁוּם שֵׁם רָע.** שֶׁיָּצָא עָלֶיהָ לַעַז זְנוּת:


משום שם רע – talk casting aspersion against her [on account of] unchastity


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:7:2)


**מִשּׁוּם נֶדֶר.** שֶׁנָּדְרָה וְהוּא אוֹמֵר אִי אֶפְשִׁי בְּאִשָּׁה נַדְרָנִית:


משום נדר – that she took an vow and he [i.e., her husband] said: It is impossible for me [to be married] to a woman who takes vows.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:7:3)


**לֹא יַחֲזִיר.** וַאֲפִלּוּ נִמְצְאוּ הַדְּבָרִים שֶׁקֶר, אוֹ הַנֶּדֶר הִתִּירוֹ חָכָם. שֶׁמָּא תֵּלֵךְ וְתִנָּשֵׂא לְאַחֵר וְיִמָּצֵא הַלַּעַז שֶׁל זְנוּת שֶׁקֶר, וְהַנֶּדֶר יַתִּירֵנוּ חָכָם וְהִיא לֹא תִהְיֶה פְּרוּצָה בִּנְדָרִים, וְיֹאמַר אִלּוּ הָיִיתִי יוֹדֵעַ שֶׁכֵּן אֲפִלּוּ הָיוּ נוֹתְנִים לִי מֵאָה מָנֶה לֹא הָיִיתִי מְגָרְשָׁהּ, וְנִמְצָא גֵּט בָּטֵל וּבָנֶיהָ מַמְזֵרִים. לְפִיכָךְ אוֹמְרִים לוֹ הֱוֵי יוֹדֵעַ שֶׁהַמּוֹצִיא אֶת אִשְׁתּוֹ מִשּׁוּם שֵׁם רַע וּמִשּׁוּם נֶדֶר לֹא יַחֲזִיר עוֹלָמִית, וּמִתּוֹךְ הַדְּבָרִים הַלָּלוּ הוּא גּוֹמֵר וּמְגָרֵשׁ גֵּרוּשִׁין גְּמוּרִים וְלֹא מָצֵי תוּ לְקַלְקְלָהּ:


לא יחזיר – And even if it were found that the words [against her] were false, or that the vow was released by a Sage, lest she go and marry another and it is found that the words of aspersion against her [on account of] unchastity were found to be false, and that her vow would be released by a Sage, and she would not be considered unrestrained in taking vows, and he [i.e. the husband] would say: If I had known that this was the case, even if they gave me one-hundred Maneh, I would not divorce her, and the Jewish bill of divorce would be considered void and her children would be illegitimate. Therefore, we say to him: Note that one who dismisses his wife because of an evil name or because of a vow cannot remarry [her] ever. And because of these words, he completes her divorce’ and no one is able to further disgrace her through immorality.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:7:4)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר כָּל נֶדֶר שֶׁיָּדְעוּ בוֹ רַבִּים לֹא יַחֲזִיר.** רַבִּי יְהוּדָה סָבַר דְּטַעְמָא דְאָמוּר רַבָּנָן הַמּוֹצִיא אֶת אִשְׁתּוֹ מִשּׁוּם שֵׁם רַע וּמִשּׁוּם נֶדֶר לֹא יַחֲזִיר, כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא תִהְיֶינָה בְּנוֹת יִשְׂרָאֵל פְּרוּצוֹת בָּעֲרָיוֹת וּבִנְדָרִים. וּמִשּׁוּם הָכִי קָאָמַר דְּנֶדֶר שֶׁיָּדְעוּ בוֹ רַבִּים, עֲשָׂרָה מִיִּשְׂרָאֵל אוֹ יוֹתֵר, אִיכָּא פְּרִיצוּתָא טְפֵי, וּקְנָסוּהָ וְלֹא יַחֲזִיר. וְשֶׁלֹּא יָדְעוּ בוֹ רַבִּים לֵיכָּא פְּרִיצוּתָא כֻּלֵּי הַאי וְלֹא קְנָסוּהָ:


רבי יהודה אומר: כל נדר שידעו בו רבים לא יחזיר – Rabbi Yehuda thinks that the reason that what the Rabbis said – that a person who dismisses {i.e, divorce) his wife because of a bad name or because of a vow, should not remarry her in order that Jewish women should not be loose in moral conduct or in vows (Gittin 46a), and because of this, it is stated that a vow that the public knew about – ten or more Israelites [involved], there is greater licentiousness, and they fine her and he cannot remarry her. But, if the public did not know about it, there is no licentiousness at all and they do not fine her.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:7:5)


**לֹא יַחֲזִיר.** דְּרַבִּי מֵאִיר סָבַר טַעְמָא מִשּׁוּם קִלְקוּלָא. הִלְכָּךְ נֶדֶר שֶׁאֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לְהָפֵר אֲבָל חָכָם יָכוֹל לְהַתִּיר, יָכוֹל הוּא לְקַלְקֵל אֶת הַגֵּט לְאַחַר שֶׁתִּנָּשֵׂא לְאַחֵר וְיֹאמַר אִלּוּ הָיִיתִי יוֹדֵעַ שֶׁחָכָם יָכוֹל לְהַתִּיר לֹא הָיִיתִי מְגָרְשָׁהּ:


לא יחזיר – since Rabbi Meir thinks that the reason is because of moral corruption. Therefore, a vow which cannot be annulled but that a Sage can release it, he [i.e., the husband] can corrupt the Jewish bill of divorce after she has married another [man] and say: If had known that a Sage can release it [the vow, I would not have divorced her.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:7:6)


**וְשֶׁאֵינוֹ צָרִיךְ חֲקִירַת חָכָם.** אֶלָּא הוּא יָכוֹל לְהָפֵר, לֹא הֻצְרְכוּ חֲכָמִים לֶאֱסֹר עָלָיו מִלְּהַחְזִירָהּ, לְפִי שֶׁאֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לְקַלְקְלָהּ וְלוֹמַר אִלּוּ הָיִיתִי יוֹדֵעַ כוּ', שֶׁהֲרֵי נֶדֶר פָּתוּחַ הוּא וְהָיָה לוֹ לְהָפֵר וְלֹא הֵפֵר:


ושאינו צריך חקירת חכם – but rather he can annul it [the vow] and there would be no need for the Sages to prohibit him from remarrying her, because he is not able to damage her and say, “If I had known etc.,” for it is an open vow and he can either annul it or not annul it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:7:7)


**לֹא אָסְרוּ זֶה.** שֶׁצָּרִיךְ חֲקִירַת חָכָם, לְהַחֲזִיר:


לא אסרו זה – that requires the study of a Sage [to find the means of absolving the vow to permit him] to remarry [her].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:7:8)


**אֶלָּא מִפְּנֵי זֶה.** שֶׁאֵינוֹ צָרִיךְ. דִּבְצָרִיךְ לֹא הָיָה לָנוּ לָחוּשׁ לְקִלְקוּלָא, לְפִי שֶׁאֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לוֹמַר אִלּוּ הָיִיתִי יוֹדֵעַ שֶׁחָכָם יָכוֹל לְהַתִּירוֹ לֹא הָיִיתִי מְגָרֵשׁ, דַּאֲנַן סַהֲדֵי שֶׁאַף עַל פִּי כֵן הָיָה מְגָרֵשׁ, שֶׁאֵין אָדָם רוֹצֶה שֶׁתִּתְבַּזֶּה אִשְׁתּוֹ בְּבֵית דִּין בִּפְנֵי חָכָם לֵילֵךְ לְבֵית דִּינוֹ וְלִשְׁאֹל עַל נִדְרָהּ. אֶלָּא מִפְּנֵי נֶדֶר שֶׁאֵינוֹ צָרִיךְ חָכָם וּבַעַל יָכוֹל לְהָפֵר, אָסְרוּ אֶת כֻּלָּן, שֶׁלֹּא יֹאמַר אִלּוּ הָיִיתִי יוֹדֵעַ שֶׁהָיִיתִי יָכוֹל לְהָפֵר לֹא הָיִיתִי מְגָרְשָׁהּ:


אלא מפני זה – that it is not necessary, for had it been necessary, we would not have to worry about disgracing her by immorality, since he would not be able to say: Had I known that a Sage can release it [i.e., the vow] , I would not have divorced [her], for we testify that even though this is the case, he would divorce her, and a man does not want to have his wife despised/humbled in the Jewish court in front of a Sage where she would go to the court and make petition about her vow, but because a vow that does not require a Sage [to release her] and the husband can annul it, they forbade all of them so that he would not say: “If I had known that I could annul it, it would not have divorced her.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:7:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:7:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:7:9)


**אָמַר רַבִּי יוֹסֵי בְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה כוּ'.** בַּגְּמָרָא מְפָרֵשׁ דַּחֲסוּרֵי מִחַסְּרָא וְהָכִי קָתָנֵי, בַּמֶּה דְּבָרִים אֲמוּרִים, כְּשֶׁנָּדְרָה הִיא, אֲבָל נָדַר הוּא שֶׁיְּגָרְשֶׁנָהּ וְגֵרְשָׁהּ, יַחֲזִיר וְלֹא חָיְשִׁינַן לְקִלְקוּלָא:


א"ר יוסי ברבי יהודה כו' In the Gemara it explains that the Mishnah is deficient (i.e., missing something) and it should be taught as follows: What are we speaking about: When she made the vow, but if he made the vow that he would divorce her and he divorced her, he may remarry her and we don’t worry about her degeneracy.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:7:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:7:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:7:10)


**וְאָמַר רַבִּי יוֹסֵי בְרַבִּי יְהוּדָה מַעֲשֶׂה נַמִּי בְּצַיְּדָן בְּאֶחָד שֶׁאָמַר לְאִשְׁתּוֹ קוֹנָם אִם אֵינִי מְגָרְשֵׁךְ.** כְּלוֹמַר יֵאָסְרוּ כָּל פֵּרוֹת שֶׁבָּעוֹלָם עָלַי אִם אֵינִי מְגָרְשֵׁךְ, וְגֵרְשָׁהּ, וְהִתִּירוּ לוֹ חֲכָמִים שֶׁיַּחֲזִירֶנָּה:


ואמר ר' יוסי ברבי יהודה: מעשה נמי בצידן באחד שאמר לאשתו קונם אם איני מגרשך – that is to say, that all the fruit that is in the world will be forbidden to me if I do not divorce you, and he divorced her, but the Sages ruled leniently to him that he may remarry her.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:7:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:7:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:7:11)


**מִפְּנֵי תִקּוּן הָעוֹלָם.** כְּלוֹמַר שֶׁלֹּא אָמְרוּ חֲכָמִים הַמְּגָרֵשׁ אֶת אִשְׁתּוֹ מִשּׁוּם נֶדֶר לֹא יַחֲזִירֶנָּה אֶלָּא מִפְּנֵי תִקּוּן הָעוֹלָם דְּחָיְשִׁינַן לְקִלְקוּלָא, וְהָא לֹא שַׁיָּךְ אֶלָּא מִשּׁוּם נֶדֶר דִּידָהּ, אֲבָל כְּשֶׁנָּדַר הוּא שֶׁאֵין כָּאן מִשּׁוּם תִּקּוּן הָעוֹלָם, הִתִּירוּ לְהַחֲזִיר. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


מפני תקון העולם – that is to say, that the Sages did not say that a man who divorces his wife because of a vow should not remarry her other than because of the sake of the social order for we fear for moral degeneracy, but that does not belong other than on account of a vow she took , but if he took a vow, and there is nothing here [concerning] the sake of the social order, they permitted him to remarry her. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:8:1)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר לֹא יַחֲזִיר.** שֶׁמָּא תִנָּשֵׂא וְתֵלֵד וְיֹאמַר אִלּוּ הָיִיתִי יוֹדֵעַ שֶׁכֵּן, אֲפִלּוּ הָיוּ נוֹתְנִים לִי מֵאָה מָנֶה לֹא הָיִיתִי מְגָרְשֵׁךְ:


רבי יהודה אומר לא יחזיר – Lest she marry [another man] and give birth [to a child] and he (i.e., the first husband] would say: had I known that this is the case, even if they would have given me one-hundred Maneh, I would not divorce you.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:8:2)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים יַחֲזִיר.** שֶׁלֹּא חָיְשִׁינַן לְקִלְקוּלָא. וּבַגְּמָרָא מְפָרֵשׁ מַאן חֲכָמִים, רַבִּי מֵאִיר, דְּאָמַר בָּעִינַן תְּנַאי כָּפוּל. וְהָכָא בְּמַאי עָסְקִינַן דְּלֹא כַּפְלֵיהּ לִתְנָאֵיהּ, שֶׁלֹּא אָמַר לָהּ הָוֵי יוֹדַעַת שֶׁמִּשּׁוּם אַיְלוֹנִית אֲנִי מוֹצִיאֵךְ, וְאִם אֵין אַתְּ אַיְלוֹנִית לֹא יְהֵא גֵּט, דְּהַשְׁתָּא הָוֵי גֵּט אֲפִלּוּ אֵינָהּ אַיְלוֹנִית:


וחכמים אומרים: יחזיר – for they were not concerned for her degeneracy, and in the Gemara, it explains who the Sages are: Rabbi Meir – who stated that we require a double condition and this is what we are dealing with – that he did not make a double condition, for he [i.e., the husband] did not say to her: “You should know that since you are incapable of conception, I am releasing you [from being my wife], and if you are not incapable of conception, it is not a Jewish bill of divorce, and now it is a Jewish bill of divorce even if she is not incapable of conception.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:8:3)


**וְהִיא תוֹבַעַת כְּתֻבָּתָהּ.** שֶׁהָאַיְלוֹנִית אֵין לָהּ כְּתֻבָּה. וְעַכְשָׁיו שֶׁנִּמְצָא שֶׁאֵינָהּ אַיְלוֹנִית, תּוֹבַעַת כְּתֻבָּתָהּ:


והיא תובעת כתובתה – a woman incapable of conception has no [rights regarding her] Ketubah/Jewish marriage contract , and now that she was found to not be incapable of conception, she is demanding [payment of] her Ketubah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:8:4)


**שְׁתִיקוּתִיךְ יָפֶה לִיךְ מִדִּבּוּרִיךָ.** שֶׁיֹּאמַר אִלּוּ יָדַעְתִּי שֶׁסּוֹפִי לִתֵּן לָךְ כְּתֻבָּה לֹא גֵּרַשְׁתִּיךְ, נִמְצָא גֵּט בָּטֵל וּבָנֶיהָ מַמְזֵרִים:


שתיקותיך יפה ליך מדבוריך – that he would say: If I had known that in the end, I would have to give you your Ketubah [payment], I would not have divorced you, and it is found that the Jewish bill of divorce is voided and her children are illegitimate.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:9:1)


**אֵין פּוֹדִין אוֹתוֹ.** וְהוּא שֶׁרָגִיל בְּכָךְ. כְּגוֹן שֶׁמָּכַר וְשָׁנָה וְשִׁלֵּשׁ:


אין פודין אותו – and he is accustomed to it, such as the case where he sold and resold and sold a third time.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:9:2)


**לוֹקֵחַ וּמֵבִיא בִכּוּרִים.** בְּכָל שָׁנָה צָרִיךְ לִקַּח מֵהַנָּכְרִי בִּכּוּרֵי פֵרוֹתֶיהָ בְּדָמִים, וּמְבִיאָן לִירוּשָׁלַיִם:


לוקח ומביא ביכורים – In each year, one must purchase from the heathen the first of its fruits with money and bring them to Jerusalem.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 4:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/4:9:3)


**מִפְּנֵי תִקּוּן הָעוֹלָם.** שֶׁלֹּא יְהֵא רָגִיל לִמְכֹּר קַרְקַע בְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל לְנָכְרִים. וְאִם מָכַר יִטְרַח לַחְזֹר וְלִפְדּוֹת:


מפני תקון העולם – so that he not be accustomed to sell land in the Land of Israel to heathens, and if he sold it, he work to return and redeem them.



## Chapter 5


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:1:1)


**הַנִּזָּקִין שָׁמִין לָהֶם בְּעִדִּית.** אַף עַל גַּב דְּמִדְּאוֹרַיְתָא הִיא, דִּכְתִיב (שמות כב) מֵיטַב שָׂדֵהוּ וּמֵיטַב כַּרְמוֹ יְשַׁלֵּם, הַאי תַּנָּא סָבַר דְּמֵיטַב שָׂדֵהוּ דְּנִזָּק קָאָמַר קְרָא, וּמִפְּנֵי תִקּוּן הָעוֹלָם אָמְרוּ חֲכָמִים שֶׁיְּשַׁלֵּם הַמַּזִּיק מֵעִדִּית שֶׁבִּנְכָסָיו, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהֵם טוֹבִים מֵעִדִּית שֶׁל נִזָּק, כְּדֵי שֶׁיְּהוּ נִזְהָרִים מִלְּהַזִּיק:


הניזקין שמין להם בעידית – Even though that it is from Torah, as it is written (Exodus 22:4): “[When a man lets his lifestock loose to graze in another’s land, and so allows a field or a vineyard to be grazed bare,] he must make restitution (from the best) for the impairment of that field or vineyard.” This Tanna [of our Mishnah] thinks that it is the best field of the one who suffered damage that is spoken of in the Biblical verse, but for the sake of the social order, the Sages said that the one who caused the damage should pay from the best of his property, even if they (i.e., his properties) are better than the best of the one who suffered the damage, in order that we should be careful from causing damage.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:1:2)


**וּבַעַל חוֹב בְּבֵינוֹנִית.** דְּמִדְּאוֹרַיְתָא לֹא גָבֵי אֶלָּא מִזִּבּוּרִית, דִּכְתִיב (דברים כד) וְהָאִישׁ אֲשֶׁר אַתָּה נוֹשֶׁה בוֹ יוֹצִיא אֵלֶיךָ אֶת הָעֲבוֹט הַחוּצָה, וְאֵין דַּרְכּוֹ שֶׁל אָדָם לְהוֹצִיא אֶלָּא הַפָּחוּת שֶׁבִּנְכָסָיו, וּמִפְּנֵי תִקּוּן הָעוֹלָם אָמְרוּ בַּעַל חוֹב בְּבֵינוֹנִית, כְּדֵי שֶׁלֹּא תִנְעֹל דֶּלֶת בִּפְנֵי לֹוִין:


ובעל חוב בבינונית – that from the Torah he does not collect other than from the lowest quality land of the estate, as it is written (Deuteronomy 24:11): “[You must remain outside,] while the man to whom you made the loan brings the pledge out to you.” But it is not the manner of a person to bring out other than the worst of his possessions, but for the sake of the social order, they [i.e., the Sages] said that the borrower pays with his middle-range [property, in order that we not shut the door to borrowers.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:1:3)


**וּכְתֻבַּת אִשָּׁה בְּזִבּוּרִית.** דְּלֵיכָּא לְמֵיחַשׁ בָּהּ לִנְעִילַת דֶּלֶת. דְּיוֹתֵר מִמַּה שֶּׁהָאִישׁ רוֹצֶה לִשָּׂא, אִשָּׁה רוֹצָה לְהִנָּשֵׂא:


וכתובת אשה בזבורית – for we do not suspect her to shut the door, for more than a man wants to marry, a woman wants to be married.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:1:4)


**רַבִּי מֵאִיר אוֹמֵר כוּ'.** וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי מֵאִיר:


ר"מ אומר כו'- The Halakha is not according to Rabbi Meir.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:2:1)


**אֵין נִפְרָעִים מִנְּכָסִים מְשֻׁעְבָּדִים.** כְּגוֹן לֹוֶה שֶׁמָּכַר בֵּינוֹנִית שֶׁלּוֹ שֶׁהִיא מְשֻׁעְבֶּדֶת לְבַעַל חוֹב, אֵין הַבַּעַל חוֹב יָכוֹל לִטְרֹף אוֹתָהּ מִן הַלּוֹקֵחַ אִם נִשְׁאַר אֵצֶל הַלֹּוֶה נְכָסִים בְּנֵי חוֹרִין, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינָן אֶלָּא זִבּוּרִית:


אין נפרעים מכנסים משועבדים – such as a borrower who sold his middle-range [property] which was mortgaged to the creditor, the creditor cannot seize it from the purchaser if free-standing property remains with the borrower, and even though they are not other than the lowest level property.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:3:1)


**אֵין מוֹצִיאִין לַאֲכִילַת פֵּרוֹת וּלְשֶׁבַח קַרְקָעוֹת.** הַגּוֹזֵל שָׂדֶה וּמְכָרָהּ לְאַחֵר, וּזְרָעָהּ וְצָמְחָה וְעָשְׂאָה פֵּרוֹת, וּבָא נִגְזָל וּגְבָאָהּ עִם פֵּרוֹתֶיהָ מִן הַלּוֹקֵחַ, אֵינוֹ מְשַׁלֵּם לַלּוֹקֵחַ כִּי אִם הַיְּצִיאָה, וְחוֹזֵר הַלּוֹקֵחַ עַל הַמּוֹכֵר וְגוֹבֶה דְּמֵי הַקַּרְקַע מִנְּכָסִים מְשֻׁעְבָּדִים, שֶׁהֲרֵי מְכָרָהּ לוֹ בְּאַחֲרָיוּת וְכָתַב לוֹ שְׁטַר מְכִירָה וַהֲרֵי הִיא מִלְוֶה בִשְׁטָר, וְאֶת הַפֵּרוֹת מִנְּכָסִים בְּנֵי חוֹרִין וְלֹא מִמְּשֻׁעְבָּדִים:


אין מוציאין לאכילת פירות ולשבח קרקעות – The one who steals a field and sells it to another [person], who sowed it and caused it to grow and made fruits and the person from whom it was stolen comes and collects it with its fruits from the purchaser, does not pay the purchaser other than the expenses [laid out following the purchase] and the purchaser goes back to the seller and collects the cost of the land from his mortgaged possessions, for he [the thief] sold it to him [the purchaser who was unaware that the land had been stolen] with surety and wrote him a document of sale , and it is a loan document, and the fruit come from free-standing property and not from mortgaged property.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:3:2)


**וְכֵן לְשֶׁבַח קַרְקָעוֹת.** אִם הִשְׁבִּיחָהּ הַלּוֹקֵחַ בִּנְטִיעַת הָאִילָנוֹת אוֹ בְּזֶבֶל וְכַיּוֹצֵא בָזֶה:


לשבח קרקעות – and similarly, if the purchase increased the value of the property by planting trees or manuring it or doing similar things.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:3:3)


**וְלִמְזוֹן הָאִשָּׁה וְהַבָּנוֹת.** דִּתְנַאי כְּתֻבָּה הוּא, וְאַתְּ תְּהֵא יָתְבָא בְּבֵיתִי וּמִתְּזְנָא מִנְּכָסַי, וּבְנָן נוּקְבִין דִּיְהָוְיָן לִיכִי מִנָּאִי יְהָוְיָן יַתְבִין בְּבֵיתִי וּמִתְּזְנִין מִנְּכָסַי וְכוּ', כְּשֶׁבָּאוֹת לִגְבּוֹת מְזוֹנוֹתֵיהֶן אֵין גּוֹבִים אֶלָּא מִנְּכָסִים בְּנֵי חוֹרִין וְלֹא מִמְּשֻׁעְבָּדִים:


ולמזון אשה והבנות – for it is a condition of the Jewish marriage contract, “for you will dwell in my house and be supported from my possessions, and the female issue that you will have from me will dwell in my house and be supported from my possessions, etc., and when they come to claim their food, they do not collect it other than from free-standing property and not from mortgaged [properties].”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:3:4)


**מִפְּנֵי תִקּוּן הָעוֹלָם.** שֶׁהֵם דְּבָרִים שֶׁאֵין לָהֶם קִצְבָה וְלֹא יֵדַע כַּמָּה הֵם וְלֹא מָצֵי מִזְדַּהֵר:


מפני תקון העולם – for they are many which have no limit and no one knows how much they are and one is not able to be careful with this.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:3:5)


**וְהַמּוֹצֵא מְצִיאָה.** וְהֶחְזִירָהּ, וְהַבְּעָלִים אוֹמְרִים שֶׁלֹּא הֶחְזִיר כֻּלָּהּ:


והמוצא מציאה – and returned [the lost object], and the owners say that the entirety was not returned.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:3:6)


**לֹא יִשָּׁבַע מִפְּנֵי תִקּוּן הָעוֹלָם.** שֶׁאִם אַתָּה אוֹמֵר יִשָּׁבַע, אֵין לְךָ אָדָם שֶׁמְּטַפֵּל בִּמְצִיאָה לְהַחְזִירָהּ:


לא ישבע מפני תקון העולם – for if you say that he should take an oath, there are no individuals who deals with the return of lost objects.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:4:1)


**יְתוֹמִים שֶׁסָּמְכוּ אֵצֶל בַּעַל הַבַּיִת.** לַעֲשׂוֹת מַעֲשֵׂיהֶם עַל פִּיו, וְלֹא שֶׁנִּתְמַנָּה לָהֶם אַפּוֹטְרוֹפּוֹס, אֲפִלּוּ הָכִי כְּאַפּוֹטְרוֹפּוֹס הוּא.


יתומים שסמכו אצל בעל הבית – to do their work at his direction, for an administrator/guardian had not been appointed for them; but nevertheless, he was like an administrator.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:4:2)


**אַפּוֹטְרוֹפּוֹס.** בִּלְשׁוֹן רוֹמִי קוֹרִין לָאָב פאט"ר וְלַקְּטַנִּים פוטו"ס. וּפֵרוּשׁ אַפּוֹטְרוֹפּוֹס אֲבִיהֶן שֶׁל קְטַנִּים:


אפוטרופוס – In the Roman language they would call a father PATER and children [would be called] POTOS. An explanation of APOTROPOS is the father of minor children.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:4:3)


**שֶׁמִּנָּהוּ אֲבִי יְתוֹמִים יִשָּׁבֵעַ.** דְּאִי לָאו דְּאִית לֵיהּ הַנָּאָה מִנֵּיהּ לֹא הֲוָה לֵיהּ אַפּוֹטְרוֹפּוֹס, וּמִשּׁוּם שְׁבוּעָה לֹא אָתֵי לְאַמְּנוּעֵי:


שמינהו אבי יתומים ישבע – for if not [i.e. if he did not take an oath], that he had benefit from him, he would not have had an administrator, and because of the oath taken, he might shrink from serving as a guardian.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:4:4)


**מִנָּהוּ בֵית דִּין לֹא יִשָּׁבֵעַ.** דְּטוֹבַת חִנָּם הִיא שֶׁזֶּה עוֹשֶׂה לְבֵית דִּין לְקַבֵּל דִּבְרֵיהֶם וְלִטְרֹחַ חִנָּם, וְאִי רָמִית עֲלֵיהּ שְׁבוּעָה אָתֵי לְאִמָּנוּעֵי:


מנוהו בית דין לא ישבע – as a gratuitous favor, as he does this for the Jewish court to accept their words and to trouble himself without pay, and if they bring a dispute [by requiring that] he take an oath [that he was faithful to his task], he can shrink from it [i.e., becoming a guardian].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:4:5)


**אַבָּא שָׁאוּל אוֹמֵר חִלּוּף הַדְּבָרִים.** מִנָּהוּ בֵּית דִּין יִשָּׁבֵעַ, דִּבְהַהִיא הֲנָאָה דְקָא נָפֵיק עֲלֵיהּ קָלָא דֶאֱינַשׁ מְהֵימָנָא הוּא דְּהָא סָמְכֵי עֲלֵיהּ בֵּי דִינָא, מִשּׁוּם שְׁבוּעָה לֹא אָתֵי לְאִמָּנוּעֵי. מִנָּהוּ אֲבִי יְתוֹמִים לֹא יִשָּׁבֵעַ, דְּטוֹבַת חִנָּם עוֹשֶׂה לוֹ לִטְרֹחַ עִם בָּנָיו, וְאִי רָמִית עֲלֵיהּ שְׁבוּעָה אָתֵי לְאִמָּנוּעֵי. וַהֲלָכָה כְּאַבָּא שָׁאוּל:


אבא שאול אומר חילוף הדברים – if he court appointed him, he should take an oath, since the benefit [he receives] has a voice that he is an honorable person [by not stealing from the funds in his care] for the Jewish court relies upon him and because of the oath taken, he will not shrink [from his duties in becoming a guardian]; [on the other hand], if the father of the orphans appointed him, he should not take an oath, as it is a gratuitous favor that he does for him to go to the trouble [of caring for] his children, and if they were to raise a dispute against him [by requiring that] he take an oath, he would shrink from his duties [of serving as a guardian]. And the Halakha is according to Abba Shaul.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:4:6)


**הַמְטַמֵּא.** טָהֳרוֹתָיו שֶׁל חֲבֵרוֹ:


המטמא – [he defiles] the pure objects of his fellow.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:4:7)


**וְהַמְדַמֵּעַ.** מְעָרֵב תְּרוּמָה בְּחֻלִּין שֶׁל חֲבֵרוֹ, וּמַפְסִידוֹ, שֶׁצָּרִיךְ לְמָכְרוֹ לַכֹּהֲנִים בְּזוֹל:


המדמע – he mixes the heave-offering/sacred donation for the Kohanim with unconsecrated foods and causes it to lose value, as it is necessary to sell it to the Kohanim cheaply.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:4:8)


**וְהַמְנַסֵּךְ.** שֶׁמְּעָרֵב יֵין נֶסֶךְ בְּיַיִן כָּשֵׁר וְאוֹסְרוֹ בַּהֲנָאָה:


המנסך – mixing wine poured for libations with kosher wine and it is forbidden to derive benefit [from it].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:4:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:4:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:4:9)


**בְּמֵזִיד חַיָּב.** וּבְדִין הוּא שֶׁיְּהֵא פָטוּר, דְּהֶזֵּק שֶׁאֵינוֹ נִכָּר לָאו שְׁמֵיהּ הֶזֵּק, אֶלָּא מִפְּנֵי תִּקּוּן הָעוֹלָם, שֶׁלֹּא יְהֵא כָּל אֶחָד וְאֶחָד הוֹלֵךְ וּמְטַמֵּא טָהֳרוֹתָיו שֶׁל חֲבֵרוֹ וְאוֹמֵר פָּטוּר אֲנִי:


במזיד חייב – but by law, he would be exempt, for damage that is not discernible in the object itself is not itself called damage (see Talmud Gittin 53a), but for the sake of the social order [we do not do this], so that every person would not go and defile his fellow’s pure objects, and say that I am exempt.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:4:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:4:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:4:10)


**הַכֹּהֲנִים שֶׁפִּגְלוּ.** קָרְבָּנוֹת, שֶׁשָּׁחֲטוּ וְזָרְקוּ דָמָם בְּמַחֲשֶׁבֶת לֶאֱכֹל מֵהֶם חוּץ לִזְמַנּוֹ. וְלֹא הוּרְצוּ לַבְּעָלִים:


הכהנים שפיגלו – [because of an improper intention in the mind of the officiating Kohen making] the sacrifices [rejectable] in that they slaughtered [the animals] and sprinkled their blood with the thought of eating from them at an inappropriate time, and it as not acceptable to the owners [of the sacrifices].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:4:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:4:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:4:11)


**מְזִידִים.** שֶׁיָּדְעוּ שֶׁפְּסוּלִים בְּכָךְ:


מזידים – that they knew that they would be invalid by doing this.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:4:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:4:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:4:12)


**חַיָּבִים.** לְשַׁלֵּם דְּמֵיהֶן לַבְּעָלִים, שֶׁהֲרֵי צְרִיכִין לְהָבִיא אֲחֵרִים, וְאִי נַמִּי נְדָבָה הִיא שֶׁאֵינוֹ חַיָּב בְּתַשְׁלוּמִין, מִכָּל מָקוֹם קָשֶׁה בְּעֵינָיו שֶׁלֹּא הִקְרִיב קָרְבָּנוֹ שֶׁהֲרֵי לְהָבִיא דּוֹרוֹן הָיָה מְבַקֵּשׁ:


חייבים – to pay their value to the owners, for they would have to bring other [sacrifices]; alternatively, it is a donation and is not obligated for indemnity payment. Nevertheless, it is difficult in his eyes that he would not make the sacrificial offering, for he was requested to bring a gift.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:5:1)


**עַל הַחֵרֶשֶׁת שֶׁהִשִּׂיאָהּ אָבִיהָ.** וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּהַוְיָא אֵשֶׁת אִישׁ גְּמוּרָה שֶׁהֲרֵי קִבֵּל אָבִיהָ קִדּוּשֶׁיהָ כְּשֶׁהִיא קְטַנָּה, אֲפִלּוּ הָכִי יוֹצְאָה בְּגֵט, וּמְקַבֶּלֶת אֶת גִּטָּהּ כְּשֶׁהִיא חֵרֶשֶׁת, וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּלֵית בַּהּ דַּעְתָּא, דְּאִשָּׁה מִתְגָּרֶשֶׁת בְּעַל כָּרְחָהּ, הִלְכָּךְ לֹא בָּעִינַן דַּעְתָּהּ:


על החרשת שהשיאה אביה – and even though she is a completely married woman, for her father had received her betrothal when she was a child/minor, even so she goes free with a Jewish bill of divorce and receives her Jewish bill of divorce while she is deaf-mute; and even though she lacks “knowledge,” since a woman is divorced against her will, therefore, we don’t require “knowledge.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:5:2)


**וְעַל קְטַנָּה בַת יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁנִּשֵּׂאת לְכֹהֵן.** וְהִיא יְתוֹמָה, דְּלֹא הָוֵי נִשּׂוּאִים אֶלָּא מִדְּרַבָּנָן:


וכל קטנה בת ישראל שנשאת לכהן – and she is an orphan, and her marriage is not valid other than according to the Rabbis.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:5:3)


**שֶׁאוֹכֶלֶת בַּתְּרוּמָה.** דְּרַבָּנָן. וְלֹא גָזְרִינַן תְּרוּמָה דְּרַבָּנָן אַטּוּ תְּרוּמָה דְּאוֹרַיְתָא:


שאוכלת בתרומה – according to the Rabbis, for we do not make the decree that [eating] Terumah/heave-offering according to the Rabbis is like [eating] Terumah of the Torah-law.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:5:4)


**מָרִישׁ.** קוֹרָה:


מריש – beam


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:5:5)


**בִּירָה.** בַּיִת גָּדוֹל:


בירה – a large house


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:5:6)


**מִפְּנֵי תַקָּנַת הַשָּׁבִים.** שֶׁאִם אַתָּה מַצְרִיכוֹ לְקַעְקֵעַ בִּירָתוֹ וּלְהַחְזִיר הַמָּרִישׁ עַצְמוֹ, יִמָּנַע מִלַּעֲשׂוֹת תְּשׁוּבָה:


מפני תקנת השבים – for it you require him [i.e., the thief] to take down (literally, “exterminate”) his home (or an entire group of buildings) and return the [stolen] beam itself (see Talmud Gittin 47a), he would be prevented from repenting.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:5:7)


**שֶׁלֹּא נוֹדְעָה לָרַבִּים.** שֶׁהִיא גְזוּלָה:


שלא נודעה לרבים – that t was stolen


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:5:8)


**שֶׁהִיא מְכַפֶּרֶת.** וְאֵין צָרִיךְ לְהָבִיא אַחֶרֶת:


שהיא מכפרת – and there is no need to bring another.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:5:9)


**מִפְּנֵי תִקּוּן מִזְבֵּחַ.** שֶׁלֹּא יִהְיוּ כֹּהֲנִים עֲצֵבִים שֶׁאָכְלוּ חֻלִּין שֶׁנִּשְׁחֲטוּ בָּעֲזָרָה, וְנִמְצָא מִזְבֵּחַ בָּטֵל, שֶׁנִּמְנָעִים מִלַּעֲבֹד עֲבוֹדָה:


מפני תקון מזבח – so that the Kohanim will not be sad that they unconsecrated meat that was slaughtered in the Temple courtyard, and it would lead to the altar being void, that they were prevented from performing the Temple ritual.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:6:1)


**לֹא הָיָה סִיקָרִיקוֹן.** נָכְרִי רוֹצֵחַ. כְּלוֹמַר לֹא דָּנוּ דִּין סִיקָרִיקוֹן, לוֹמַר שֶׁהַקּוֹנֶה קַרְקַע שֶׁל יִשְׂרָאֵל מִיַּד הַנָּכְרִי רוֹצֵחַ, יִהְיֶה חַיָּב לִדּוֹן עִם הַבְּעָלִים:


לא היה סקריקון – a murdering heathen, that is do say, that they did not adjudicate the law concerning the purchase of confiscated property (see Talmud Gittin 55b) to say that whomever purchases Jewish land from a murdering heathen, will be liable to adjudicate with the owners.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:6:2)


**בִּשְׁעַת הֲרוּגֵי הַמִּלְחָמָה.** בְּשָׁעָה שֶׁהָיְתָה הַגְּזֵרָה קָשָׁה עַל יִשְׂרָאֵל לֵהָרֵג בַּמִּלְחָמָה. שֶׁהַלּוֹקֵחַ מִמֶּנּוּ בְּאוֹתָהּ שָׁעָה הָיָה מִקָּחוֹ קַיָּם וְלֹא הָיָה צָרִיךְ לִדּוֹן עִם הַיִשְׂרָאֵל בַּעַל הַקַּרְקַע, מִשּׁוּם דְּאַגַּב אוֹנְסֵיהּ דְּיִשְׂרָאֵל הֲוָה גָּמַר וּמַקְנֶה לְסִיקָרִיקוֹן. וְקָיְמָא לָן [בָּבָא בַתְרָא מז:] תָּלוּהוּ וְזָבִין זְבִינֵיהּ זְבִינֵי:


משעת הרוגי מלחמה – At the time when the decree was difficult for the Jews [that they would be] killed during warfare, for a person who buys from him at that time, his purchase is valid, and there was no need to adjudicate with an Israelite who owns the land, since as a result of unavoidable compulsion where an Israelite would complete a sale to the heathen murderers, and we hold (Talmud Bava Batra 47b): “If a person consents to sell something through fear of physical violence (literally, “if they hang him and he sells”), the sale is valid.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:6:3)


**אֲבָל מֵהֲרוּגֵי מִלְחָמָה וְאֵילָךְ.** שֶׁלֹּא הָיְתָה הַגְּזֵרָה לֵהָרֵג, דָּנוּ דִּין סִיקָרִיקוֹן, לוֹמַר שֶׁהַקּוֹנֶה מִמֶּנּוּ יַעֲשֶׂה דִּין עִם הַבְּעָלִים. כְּמוֹ שֶׁמְּפֹרָשׁ בַּמִּשְׁנָה:


אבל מהרוגי המלחמה ואילך – when there was no decree to be killed, they adjudicated the law concerning the purchase of confiscated propery, to say that when he purchases it from him, they should do a judgment with the owners as it is explained in the Mishnah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:6:4)


**מִקָּחוֹ בָטֵל.** דְּאָמְרִינַן מִיִּרְאָה עָבַד:


מקחו בטל – for as we say that he did it out of fear.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:6:5)


**לָקַח מִן הָאִישׁ.** קַרְקַע הַמְיֻחָד לִכְתֻבַּת אִשְׁתּוֹ:


לקח מן האיש – land that is designated for his wife’s Ketubah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:6:6)


**מִקָּחוֹ בָטֵל.** דְּאָמְרָה נַחַת רוּחַ עָשִׂיתִי לְבַעֲלִי:


מקחו בטל – for she said that I did it only to gratify my husband (but did not mean to sell – see Talmud Ketubot 95a).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:6:7)


**נוֹתֵן לַבְּעָלִים רְבִיעַ.** שֶׁשִּׁעֲרוּ דְּסִיקָרִיקוֹן מִתּוֹךְ שֶׁבְּחִנָּם בָּא לְיָדוֹ מוֹזִיל גַּבֵּיהּ רִבְעָא:


נותן לבעלים רביע – that they estimated, for the heathen murderers which it had come into his hand without paying for it, he bought [the confiscated field] for one-fourth less than its real value.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:7:1)


**רוֹמֵז וְנִרְמָז.** מַה שֶּׁהוּא רוֹמֵז אוֹ שֶׁאֲחֵרִים רוֹמְזִים לוֹ וְנִתְרַצָּה, הַכֹּל קַיָּם. רְמִיזָה, בְּיָדָיו וּבְרֹאשׁוֹ. קְפִיצָה, עֲקִימַת שְׂפָתַיִם, שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (איוב ה) קָפְצָה פִּיהָ. וְאֵינוֹ סִימָן נִכָּר כְּמוֹ רְמִיזָה:


רומז ונרמז – what her [i.e., the deaf-mute] gesticulates or that others gesticulate to him and is satisfied by it, everything exists. Gesticulation is with one’s hands or with one’s head. Mimic intimation [is accomplished] by the curving of the lips, as it says (Job 5:16): “[So there is hope for the wretched;] the mouth of wrongdoing is stopped.” But mimic intimation is not recognized as is gesticulation.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:7:2)


**בְּמִטַּלְטְלִין.** אִם מָכַר מִטַּלְטְלִין. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּבֶן בְּתֵירָה:


במטלטלין – if he sold movables, and the Halakha is not according to Ben Beteira.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:7:3)


**הַפָּעוֹטוֹת.** הַקְּטַנִּים כְּבֶן שֶׁבַע כְּבֶן שְׁמֹנֶה אִם הוּא חָרִיף וְיוֹדֵעַ בְּטִיב מַשָּׂא וּמַתָּן, אוֹ כְּבֶן תֵּשַׁע וּבֶן עֶשֶׂר אִם אֵינוֹ חָרִיף כָּל כָּךְ:


הפעוטות – minors at the age of seven or eight, if he is sharp/bright and knows the nature of business relationships, or if he the age of nine or ten and is not so sharp.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:7:4)


**מִקָּחָן מִקָּח וּמִמְכָּרָן מִמְכָּר בְּמִטַּלְטְלִין.** וּמַתְּנָתָן מַתָּנָה. אֶחָד מַתְּנַת בָּרִיא, וְאֶחָד מַתְּנַת שְׁכִיב מֵרַע. אֶחָד מַתָּנָה מְרֻבָּה, וְאֶחָד מַתָּנָה מֻעֶטֶת:


מקחן מקח וממכרן ממכר במטלטלין – and their gifts are gifts, whether it is a gift of a healthy individual or the gift of someone on his death-bed, whether it is a large gift or a small gift.



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:8:1)


**כֹּהֵן קוֹרֵא רִאשׁוֹן וְכוּ'.** כְּשֶׁהֵן שָׁוִין בְּחָכְמָה. אֲבָל אִם הַיִּשְׂרָאֵל גָּדוֹל בְּחָכְמָה, קוֹדֵם לַכֹּהֵן וְלַלֵּוִי, דְּמַמְזֵר תַּלְמִיד חָכָם קוֹדֵם לְכֹהֵן גָּדוֹל עַם הָאָרֶץ. הֲדֵין הוּא דִינָא דִגְמָרָא. וְהָאִידְנָא נָהוּג שֶׁכֹּהֵן אֲפִלּוּ עַם הָאָרֶץ קוֹדֵם לְחָכָם גָּדוֹל שֶׁבְּיִשְׂרָאֵל:


כהן קורא ראשון – When they are equal in wisdom, but if the Israelite is greater in wisdom, he precedes the Kohen and the Levite (see Mishnah Horayot, Chapter 3, Mishnah 8) for a Sage who is illegitimate comes before a High Priest who is an ignoramus. The law is the law of the Gemara, but today it is the practice that the Kohen, even if he is an ignoramus, comes before a great Sage who is an Israelite.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:8:2)


**מִפְּנֵי דַרְכֵי שָׁלוֹם.** דְּמִדְּאוֹרַיְתָא יָכוֹל הַכֹּהֵן לָתֵת רְשׁוּת לְמִי שֶׁיִּרְצֶה שֶׁיִּקְרָא בַּתּוֹרָה לְפָנָיו, וּמִפְּנֵי דַרְכֵי שָׁלוֹם אָמְרוּ שֶׁיִּקְרָא הוּא רִאשׁוֹן וְלֹא יִתֵּן רְשׁוּת לְאַחֵר שֶׁיִּקְרָא, דְּלֹא לֵיתֵי לְאִינְּצוֹיֵי וְלוֹמַר מִפְּנֵי מָה הִרְשָׁה לָזֶה וְלֹא לְאַחֵר. וְלֹא שְׁנָא בְּשַׁבָּתוֹת וְיָמִים טוֹבִים דִּשְׁכִיחֵי רַבִּים, וְלֹא שְׁנָא בְּשֵׁנִי וּבַחֲמִישִׁי בַּזְּמַן הַזֶּה, לְעוֹלָם כֹּהֵן קוֹרֵא רִאשׁוֹן וְאֵינוֹ יָכוֹל לָתֵת רְשׁוּת לְיִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁיִּקְרָא לְפָנָיו, כִּי הֵיכִי דְּלֹא לֵיתוּ לְאִינְּצוֹיֵי. וְאִם אֵין שָׁם כֹּהֵן, נִתְפָּרְדָה הַחֲבִילָה, וְלֹא יִקְרָא לֵוִי שֵׁנִי אֶלָּא כְּפִי חֲשִׁיבוּתוֹ, וְאִית דְּאָמְרֵי לֹא יִקְרָא לֵוִי בַּתּוֹרָה כְלָל, וְכֵן הַמִּנְהָג:


מפני דרכי שלום – that from the Torah, a Kohen can give permission to whomever he wishes that would read from the Torah before him, but for the sake of the social order, they said that he [i.e., the Kohen] should read first and not give permission to another to read [before him], so that it doesn’t come to wrangling. But if there is no Kohen there, the cord has been severed and a Levite should not read second, other than according to his importance. And there are those who say that the Levite should not [in this case where there is no Kohen] read (i.e., called up to) the Torah at all and this is the custom.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:8:3)


**מְעָרְבִין בְּבַיִת יָשָׁן.** בְּנֵי חָצֵר שֶׁרְגִילִים לִתֵּן עֵרוּבָן בְּכָל שַׁבָּת בְּבַיִת אֶחָד, אֵין מְשַׁנִּין אֶת מְקוֹמָן לִתְּנוֹ בְּבַיִת אַחֵר:


מערבין בבית ישן – members of a courtyard who are accustomed to place their Eruv (contribute their share towards a dish which is deposited in one of the dwellings, by which act all the dwellings are considered as common to all – whereby the carrying of objects on Shabbat from one to another and across the court are permitted) in one [particular] house, should not change their place to place it in another house.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:8:4)


**מִפְּנֵי דַרְכֵי שָׁלוֹם.** שֶׁבְּנֵי אָדָם שֶׁהָיוּ רְגִילִים לִרְאוֹת הָעֵרוּב בְּאוֹתוֹ בַּיִת, עַכְשָׁיו שֶׁאֵין רוֹאִים אוֹתוֹ יֹאמְרוּ שֶׁמְּטַלְטְלִין בְּלֹא עֵרוּב, וְאִיכָּא חֲשָׁדָא:


מפני דרכי שלום – that people who are accustomed to see the Eruv in the same house, [and] now that they don’t see it, will say that they are carrying [on the Sabbath] without an Eruv, and there is suspicion.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:8:5)


**בּוֹר שֶׁהוּא קָרוֹב.** לְמוֹצָא אַמַּת הַמַּיִם הַבָּא מִן הַנָּהָר:


בור שהוא קרוב – to one who finds a canal that comes from the river.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:8:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:8:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:8:6)


**מִתְמַלֵּא רִאשׁוֹן.** וְאַחֲרָיו יִתְמַלְּאוּ הַתַּחְתּוֹנִים:


מתמלא ראשון – and afterwards the lower ones are filled.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:8:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:8:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:8:7)


**מְצוּדוֹת חַיָּה.** שֶׁאֵין לָהֶם תּוֹךְ דְּלִיקְנֵי לֵיהּ כֵּלָיו:


מצודות חיה – that lack a receptacle where he acquires his utensils (?)


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:8:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:8:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:8:8)


**יֵשׁ בָּהֶן גָּזֵל מִפְּנֵי דַרְכֵי שָׁלוֹם.** וְלֹא נָפֵיק בְּדַיָּנִים:


יש בהן משום גזל מפני דרכי שלום – and is not removed by the judges


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:8:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:8:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:8:9)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי אוֹמֵר גָּזֵל גָּמוּר.** מִדִּבְרֵיהֶם. וְנָפֵיק בְּדַיָּנִים. וּמִיהוּ מוֹדֶה רַבִּי יוֹסֵי דְּלֹא הָוֵי גָּזֵל דְּאוֹרַיְתָא לַעֲבֹר עָלָיו בְּלָאו. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


רבי יוסי אומר: גזל גמור – According to the Rabbis and is removed by judges. However, Rabbi Yosi admits that it is not theft according to the Torah, to transgress with a negative commandment. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yosi.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:8:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:8:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:8:10)


**הַמְנַקֵּף.** חוֹתֵךְ. כְּמוֹ וְנָקַף סִבְכֵי הַיַּעַר (ישעיה י):


המנקף – cut off/sever, like Isaiah 10:34: “The thickets of the forest shall be hacked away with iron…”



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:9:1)


**הַחֲשׁוּדָה עַל הַשְּׁבִיעִית.** לִשְׁמֹר פֵּרוֹת שְׁבִיעִית וּלְהַצְנִיעָם מִן הַבִּעוּר וָאֵילָךְ:


החשודה על השביעית – to guard/preserve the seventh year fruit and hide them from removal [of the fruit] (in the third and sixth years of the Sabbath period) and beyond.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:9:2)


**לֹא תָבֹר וְלֹא תִטְחַן עִמָּהּ.** לְסַיְּעָהּ, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁאָסוּר לְסַיֵּעַ בַּיָּדַיִם יְדֵי עוֹבְרֵי עֲבֵרָה בִּשְׁעַת הָעֲבֵרָה:


לא תבור ולא תטחון עמה – to assist her, since it is forbidden to help with his hands those who sin while they are sinning.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:9:3)


**לְאֵשֶׁת עַם הָאָרֶץ.** הַחֲשׁוּדָה עַל הַמַּעַשְׂרוֹת:


לאשת עם הארץ – who is suspect regarding tithing


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:9:4)


**וּבוֹרֶרֶת וְטוֹחֶנֶת.** לְפִי שֶׁרֹב עַמֵּי הָאָרֶץ מְעַשְּׂרִין הֵן:


ובוררת וטוחנת – since most ignoramuses do tithe


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:9:5)


**אֲבָל מִשֶּׁתַּטִּיל הַמַּיִם.** בָּעִסָּה:


אבל משתטיל המים – in the dough


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:9:6)


**לֹא תִגַּע עִמָּהּ.** לְפִי שֶׁמִּשֶּׁגִּלְגְּלָה הֻטְבְּלָה לְחַלָּה, וְקָא מִטַּמְּאָה מֵחֲמַת כֵּלִים טְמֵאִים שֶׁהֲרֵי הֻכְשְׁרָה לְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה, וְזוֹ מְסַיְּעָהּ לְגַלְגֵּל, וְאָסוּר לִגְרֹם טֻמְאָה לַחַלָּה:


לא תגע עמה – since when she rolls it, it becomes liable for setting aside Hallah (i.e., the piece of dough taken off, thrown into the oven and burned), and she becomes defiled through impure utensils , which had become susceptible to receive impurity; and she who helps her to roll [the dough] is forbidden to cause the Hallah to become impure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:9:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:9:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:9:7)


**וְכֻלָּן לֹא אָמְרוּ כוּ'.** לֹא הִתִּירוּ לְהַשְׁאִילָם כֵּלִים וּלְסַיְּעָם בְּלֹא שְׁעַת עֲבֵרָה עַצְמָהּ, אֶלָּא מִשּׁוּם דַּרְכֵי שָׁלוֹם:


וכולן לא אמרו כו' – they did not permit them to lend utensils and assist them outside of the time of the sin itself, other than because of the ways of peace.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:9:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:9:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:9:8)


**וּמַחֲזִיקִים יְדֵי גוֹיִם.** לוֹמַר לָהֶם תֶּחֱזַקְנָה יְדֵיכֶם:


ומחזיקין ידי גוים – to tell them that they should strengthen their hands


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 5:9:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:9:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/5:9:9)


**וְשׁוֹאֲלִים בִּשְׁלוֹמָם.** כָּל הַיָּמִים וַאֲפִלּוּ בְּיוֹם חַגָּם. וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁמֵּטִיל שֵׁם שָׁמַיִם עַל הַנָּכְרִי, שֶׁשָּׁלוֹם אֶחָד מִשְּׁמוֹתָיו שֶׁל הַקָּדוֹשׁ בָּרוּךְ הוּא:


ושואלים בשלומם – all the days, even on the days of their holidays, and even though he places the name of heaven on the heathen [but nevertheless] peace is one of the names of the Holy One, blessed be He.



## Chapter 6


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 6:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:1:1)


**הָאוֹמֵר הִתְקַבֵּל.** אִם רָצָה לַחֲזֹר יַחֲזֹר. דְּגֵט חוֹב הוּא לָהּ, וְאֵין חָבִין לְאָדָם שֶׁלֹּא מִדַּעְתּוֹ:


האומר: התקבל – אם רצה לחזור יחזור – for the Jewish bill of divorce is debt that he has towards her (acting in behalf of a person to the latter’s disadvantage), and we do not do something that will do harm to a person without the other’s knowledge (see Mishnah Eruvin, Chapter 7, Mishnah 11 and Mishnah Gittin, Chapter 1, Mishnah 6).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 6:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:1:2)


**לֹא יַחֲזֹר.** דְּכֵיוָן דְּאִיהִי שַׁוִּיתֵיהּ שָׁלִיחַ הֲרֵי הוּא כְּיָדָהּ, וְנִתְגָּרְשָׁה מִיָּד בְּקַבָּלָתוֹ שֶׁל זֶה:


לא יחזור – for since she made him equivalent as her agent, behold he is like her hand and and is divorced immediately when he receives from this one [i.e., the husband].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 6:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:1:3)


**אַף הָאוֹמֶרֶת טֹל לִי גִטִּי.** לְשׁוֹן קַבָּלָה הוּא. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל:


אף האומרת טול לי גיטי – this is the language of reception. And the Halakha is according to Rabban Shimon ben Gamaliel.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 6:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:2:1)


**צְרִיכָה.** לְהָבִיא לְפָנֵינוּ שְׁתֵּי כִתֵּי עֵדִים:


צריכה – to bring before us two sets of witnesses


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 6:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:2:2)


**שְׁנַיִם שֶׁיֹּאמְרוּ בְּפָנֵינוּ אָמָרָה.** לוֹ לְקַבְּלוֹ:


שנים שיאמרו בפנינו אמרה – to him [i.e., the husband] to receive it [i.e., the Jewish bill of divorce].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 6:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:2:3)


**וּשְׁנַיִם שֶׁיֹּאמְרוּ בְּפָנֵינוּ קִבֵּל וְקָרָע.** וּבִשְׁעַת הַשְּׁמָד שָׁנוּ, שֶׁגָּזְרוּ עַל הַמִּצְוֹת וְהָיוּ קוֹרְעִים הַגֵּט מִיָּד, שֶׁלֹּא יֵרָאֶה:


ושנים שיאמרו בפנינו קבל וקרע – And this was taught during the time of religious persecution, where they decreed against [the observance of] the commandments and they would immediately tear the Jewish bill of divorce, so that they should it should not be seen.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 6:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:2:4)


**וַאֲפִלּוּ הֵן כוּ'.** שֶׁהַשְּׁנַיִם שֶׁאָמְרָה בִפְנֵיהֶם הֵם עַצְמָם רָאוּ כְּשֶׁקִּבְּלוּ:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 6:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:2:5)


**אוֹ אַחַד מִן הָרִאשׁוֹנִים וְאַחַד מִן הָאַחֲרוֹנִים וְאֶחָד.** שְׁלִישִׁי, שֶׁנַּעֲשָׂה עֵד בְּזוֹ וּבְזוֹ, מִצְטָרֵף עִמָּהֶם:


או אחד מן הראשונים ואחד מן האחרונים ואחד – a third [individual] who was made a witness with this group and the other group, who joins with them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 6:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:2:6)


**הִיא וְאָבִיהָ.** אוֹ הִיא אוֹ אָבִיהָ. הִיא יֵשׁ לָהּ יָד דְּהָא גְדוֹלָה הִיא. וְאָבִיהָ נַמִּי זַכַּאי לְקַבְּלוֹ:


היא ואביה – either she [i.e., the betrothed maiden, between the ages of twelve and twelve-and-one-half] or her father, for she has “a hand,” because she is considered an adult, and her father also has authority to receive it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 6:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:2:7)


**אֵינָהּ מִתְגָּרֶשֶׁת.** וַאֲפִלּוּ בְּקַבָּלַת אָבִיהָ, דִּכְתִיב (דברים כד) וְשִׁלְּחָהּ מִבֵּיתוֹ, מִי שֶׁמְּשַׁלְּחָהּ וְאֵינָהּ חוֹזֶרֶת, יָצְאָה זוֹ שֶׁמְּשַׁלְּחָהּ וְחוֹזֶרֶת:


אינה מתגרשת – and even with her father receiving it [i.e., the Jewish bill of divorce], as it is written (Deuteronomy 24:1): “…and sends her away from his house,” [which describes] someone who sends her away but she does not return, excluding [a case] where he sends her away but she returns.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 6:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:3:1)


**בְּמָקוֹם אַחֵר פָּסוּל.** שֶׁהַבַּעַל מַקְפִּיד, שֶׁאֵין רְצוֹנוֹ שֶׁיַּלְעִיזוּ עָלָיו שָׁם:


במקום אחר פסול – for the husband is strict that it is not his desire that they should cast suspicion upon him there.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 6:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:3:2)


**הֲרֵי הִיא בְמָקוֹם פְּלוֹנִי.** אֵינוֹ אֶלָּא כְּמַרְאֵה מָקוֹם, שָׁם תִּמְצָאֶנָּהּ:


הרי היא במקום פלומי – he [i.e., the husband] is doing nothing more than showing him the place, for there she can be found.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 6:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:3:3)


**וְרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר מַכְשִׁיר.** דְּסָבַר אִיהוּ דְמִדַּעְתֵּיהּ מְגָרֵשׁ אִיכָּא קְפֵידָא, אִיהִי דִבְעַל כָּרְחָהּ מִתְגָּרֶשֶׁת מַרְאָה מָקוֹם הִיא לוֹ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


רבי אליעזר מכשיר – for he holds that he [i.e., the husband] is divorcing [her] of his own free will, there is an intimation that you care for a thing to be exactly as you want it, for she is divorced against her will, and she is merely showing him the place. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eliezer.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 6:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:4:1)


**הָבֵא לִי גִטִּי אוֹכֶלֶת בַּתְּרוּמָה.** אִם אֵשֶׁת כֹּהֵן הִיא, עַד שֶׁיַּגִּיעַ גֵּט לְיָדָהּ:


הבא לי גטי אוכלת בתרומה – if she is the wife of a Kohen, until the Jewish bill of divorce reaches her hand.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 6:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:4:2)


**לְאוֹתוֹ מָקוֹם.** שֶׁכָּךְ אָמְרָה לוֹ לֹא תְהֵא שְׁלוּחִי אֶלָּא שָׁם:


לאותו מקום – since she said to him (i.e., the agent): you will not be my agent other than “there.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 6:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:4:3)


**וְרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אוֹסֵר מִיָּד.** מִשֶּׁפָּרַשׁ מֵאֶצְלָהּ. וְרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר לְטַעֲמֵיהּ דְּמַכְשִׁיר כְּשֶׁקִּבְּלוֹ בְּמָקוֹם אַחֵר, דְּמַרְאָה מָקוֹם הִיא לוֹ וּמִשְּׁעַת קַבָּלָה אִגָּרְשָׁה לָהּ, לְפִיכָךְ מִשָּׁעָה שֶׁפָּרַשׁ מִלְּפָנֶיהָ אֲסוּרָה, שֶׁמָּא מְצָאוֹ חוּץ לָעִיר וְקִבְּלוֹ הֵימֶנּוּ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


ורבי אליעזר אוסר מיד – from when he separated from her. And Rabbi Eliezer, according to his reasoning, who validates [the Jewish bill of divorce] when it is received in a different placer, for he she is merely showing him the place, and from the time that he [i.e., the agent] receives it, she will be divorced from him. Therefore, from the moment that he separated from her presence, she is prohibited [from consuming the heave-offering/sanctified food of the Kohen] lest the husband find the agent outside the city and he receives it [i.e., the Jewish bill of divorce] from him. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eliezer.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 6:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:5:1)


**כִּתְבוּ אִגֶּרֶת וְכוּ'.** שֶׁהַגֵּט קָרוּי אִגֶּרֶת, שֶׁכָּךְ כָּתוּב בּוֹ וְאִגֶּרֶת שְׁבוּקִין:


כתבו אגרת וכו' – For the Jewish bill of divorce is called a “letter” since [the words] “a letter of divorce” in it [i.e., the Jewish bill of divorce].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 6:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:5:2)


**פִּטְרוּהָ לֹא אָמַר כְּלוּם.** דִּלְמָא לְשׁוֹן פְּטוּר וְחוֹבָה קָאָמַר, לְהָקֵל מֵעָלֶיהָ חוֹבוֹת שֶׁחַיֶּבֶת:


פטרוה לא אמר כלום – perhaps the language of “exempt” and “liable” is being said, to lighten from her the obligations that she is liable for.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 6:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:5:3)


**פַּרְנְסוּהָ.** לְשׁוֹן עֲשִׂיַּת צְרָכֶיהָ כְּמוֹ מוֹצִיאִים לַפַּרְנָסָה וְכוּ'. הִלְכָּךְ לֹא יָדְעִינַן אִי צָרְכֵי הַגֵּט הוּא, שֶׁלֹּא תְהֵא זְקוּקָה לְיָבָם, אִי צָרְכֵי מַלְבּוּשׁ וּכְסוּת הוּא:


פרנסוה – the language of performing her needs, such as taking out for [the purposes] of [providing] sustenance etc. Therefore, we do not know if these are the needs of the Jewish bill of divorce, that she will not be dependent upon/tied to leviratical marriage (i.e., she cannot marry otherwise until released from him), or [the document is speaking of] the needs of clothing and covering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 6:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:5:4)


**כַּנִּמּוֹס.** כַּחֹק. לֹא יָדְעִינַן אִי חֹק גֵּט אִי חֹק מָזוֹן וּכְסוּת. וְכֵן כָּרָאוּי:


כנימוס – according to law, and we do not know if it is the Jewish law of divorce or the law of [provision of] food and clothing, and similarly as appropriate.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 6:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:5:5)


**הַיּוֹצֵא בְקוֹלָר.** לֵהָרֵג בְּדִין הַמַּלְכוּת:


היוצא בקולר – to be killed as per the law of the kingdom.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 6:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:5:6)


**וְאָמַר כִּתְבוּ.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא אָמַר תְּנוּ:


ואמר כתבו – even though he did not say, “give it” [to my wife].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 6:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:5:7)


**יִכְתְּבוּ וְיִתֵּנוּ.** דְּאַגַּב פַּחְדֵּיהּ טָרֵיד וְלֹא פָרֵישׁ:


יכתבו ויתנו – for on account of his fear, he was troubled and did not expound [on his intentions].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 6:5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:5:8)


**הַמְפָרֵשׁ.** לַיָּם:


המפרש – on the sea


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 6:5:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:5:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:5:9)


**וְהַיּוֹצֵא בִשְׁיָרָא.** לַמִּדְבָּרִיּוֹת:


והיוצא בשיירא – to the deserts


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 6:5:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:5:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:5:10)


**הַמְּסֻכָּן.** חוֹלֶה. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן שְׁזוּרִי:


המסוכן – the infirm, and Halakha is according to Rabbi Shimon Shezuri



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 6:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:6:1)


**וְאָמַר כָּל הַשּׁוֹמֵעַ אֶת קוֹלוֹ.** וּפֵרֵשׁ שְׁמוֹ וְשֵׁם עִירוֹ:


ואמר: כל השומע את קולו – and he mentioned his name and the name of his city


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 6:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:6:2)


**רָצָה לְשַׂחֶק בָּהּ.** הוֹאִיל וְלֹא אָמַר תְּנוּ:


רצה לשחק בה – for since he did not say, “give it”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 6:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:6:3)


**מַעֲשֶׂה בְּבָרִיא.** מְפָרֵשׁ בַּגְּמָרָא דַּחֲסוּרֵי מִחַסְּרָא וְהָכִי קָתָנֵי, וְאִם הוֹכִיחַ סוֹפוֹ עַל תְּחִלָּתוֹ, הֲרֵי זֶה גֵט, וּמַעֲשֶׂה נַמִּי בְּבָרִיא וְכוּ'. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבָּן שִׁמְעוֹן בֶּן גַּמְלִיאֵל:


מעשה בבריא – The Gemara explains that the Mishnah is deficient and this is how it should be explained: And if his end result is evidence of his [thinking at the] beginning, then it is surely a Jewish bill of divorce. And there is a story also about a healthy individual. And the Halakha is according to Rabban Shimon ben Gamaliel.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 6:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:7:1)


**אָמַר לִשְׁנַיִם תְּנוּ גֵט לְאִשְׁתִּי.** אַף עַל גַּב דְּלֹא אָמַר כִּתְבוּ וּתְנוּ, הֲרֵי אֵלּוּ יִכְתְּבוּ בְּעַצְמָן. וְלֹא יֹאמְרוּ לְסוֹפֵר אַחֵר שֶׁיִּכְתֹּב, וְלֹא לְעֵדִים לַחְתֹּם, דְּלָאו בֵּית דִּין שַׁוִּינְהוּ לְצַוּוֹת עַל אֲחֵרִים אֶלָּא עֵדִים שַׁוִּינְהוּ, וְהֵן הֵן שְׁלוּחָיו הֵן הֵן עֵדָיו. וְאִם אָמַר לִשְׁלֹשָׁה כִּתְבוּ וּתְנוּ גֵט לְאִשְׁתִּי, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהֵן רְאוּיִין לֵעָשׂוֹת בֵּית דִּין, כֵּיוָן דִּבְהֶדְיָא אָמַר לְהוּ כִּתְבוּ, לָאו בֵּית דִּין שַׁוִּינְהוּ אֶלָּא עֵדִים:


אמר לשנים תנו גט לאשתי – even though he did not say, “write and give,” they should write [it] on their own, and not tell another scribe that he should write it, nor for witnesses to attest it with their signatures, for the Jewish court did consider that they should command others, but rather [only] on the witnesses, “these are the agents,” “these are the witnesses,.” But if he [i.e., the husband] said to three [individuals]: write and give a Jewish bill of divorce to my wife,” even though they are worthy to serve as a Jewish court, since in public, he said to them, “write,” he did not compare them to a Jewish court, but rather only as witnesses.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 6:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:7:2)


**אָמַר לִשְׁלֹשָׁה תְּנוּ.** וְלֹא אָמַר כִּתְבוּ:


אמר לג' תנו – but did not say, “write”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 6:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:7:3)


**הֲרֵי אֵלּוּ יֹאמְרוּ.** לְעֵדִים אֲחֵרִים, לִכְתֹּב וְלַחְתֹּם וְלִתֵּן, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁעֲשָׂאָן בֵּית דִּין:


הרי אלו יאמרו – to other witnesses to write, affix their signatures nad give it, because they were made into a Jewish court.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 6:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:7:4)


**מִבֵּית הָאֲסוּרִים.** מִשְּׁמוֹ שֶׁל רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא שֶׁהָיָה חָבוּשׁ בְּבֵית הָאֲסוּרִים:


בבית האסורים – in the name of Rabbi Akiba who was imprisoned.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 6:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:7:5)


**נוּמֵינוּ לַשָּׁלִיחַ.** אָמַרְנוּ לְרַבִּי חֲנִינָא שֶׁנַּעֲשָׂה שָׁלִיחַ בַּהֲלָכָה זוֹ לְאָמְרָהּ בְּבֵית הַמִּדְרָשׁ:


נומינו לשליח – We said to Rabbi Hanina that we should appoint an agent with regard to this Halakha and to mention it in the Bet Midrash/academy.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 6:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/6:7:6)


**יִלְמְדוּ.** אִם אֵינָם יוֹדְעִים לִכְתֹּב. יִלְמְדוּ לִכְתֹּב עַד שֶׁיִּכְתְּבוּהוּ הֵן בְּעַצְמָן:


ילמדו – if they do not know how to write [a Jewish bill of divorce], they should learn to write until they can write them on their own.



## Chapter 7


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 7:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/7:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/7:1:1)


**מִי שֶׁאֲחָזוֹ קֻרְדְּיָקוֹס.** שֶׁנִּתְבַּלְבְּלָה דַּעְתּוֹ מֵחֲמַת שֵׁד הַשּׁוֹלֵט עַל הַשּׁוֹתֶה יַיִן חָדָשׁ:


מי שאחזו קורדייקוס – that his mind became confused as a result of a devil who reigns over those who drink new wine.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 7:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/7:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/7:1:2)


**אֵין בִּדְבָרָיו הָאַחֲרוֹנִים כְּלוּם.** וְאֵין צָרִיךְ לַחְזֹר וּלְשָׁאֲלוֹ אַחַר שֶׁנִּתְיַשְּׁבָה דַּעְתּוֹ, אֶלָּא כוֹתְבִין הַגֵּט וְסוֹמְכִים עַל דְּבָרָיו הָרִאשׁוֹנִים. וּמִיהוּ כָּל זְמַן שֶׁדַּעְתּוֹ מְבֻלְבֶּלֶת אֵין כּוֹתְבִין הַגֵּט:


אין בדבריו האחרונים כלום – And there is no need to return and request another when he was clear-minded once again, but we write the Jewish bill of divorce and rely upon his earlier words. However, as long as his mind is confused, we do not write the Jewish bill of divorce.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 7:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/7:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/7:1:3)


**הִרְכִּין.** הִטָּה:


הרכין – tilted/bent


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 7:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/7:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/7:1:4)


**בּוֹדְקִין אוֹתוֹ.** בִּדְבָרִים אֲחֵרִים:


בודקין אותו – with other words


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 7:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/7:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/7:1:5)


**הֲרֵי אֵלּוּ יִכְתְּבוּ וְיִתֵּנוּ.** אִם הִרְכִּין עַל הַגֵּט הַרְכָּנַת הֵן:


הרי אלו יכתבו ויתנו – if he inclined his head over the Jewish bill of divorce an inclination of “yes.”



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 7:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/7:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/7:2:1)


**אָמְרוּ לוֹ.** לַבָּרִיא אוֹ לִשְׁכִיב מֵרַע חָכָם:


אמרו לו – to a healthy person or to a Sage on his death-bed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 7:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/7:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/7:2:2)


**נִכְתֹּב גֵּט לְאִשְׁתֶּךָ.** שֶׁלֹּא תִּזָּקֵק לְיָבָם:


נכתוב גט לאשתך – that she should not be in need of the husband’s brother (in the case of the husband dying without issue, whose brother enters the estate and marries his wife).



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 7:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/7:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/7:3:1)


**זֶה גִטֵּךְ אִם מַתִּי.** לֹא אָמַר כְּלוּם. דְּמַשְׁמַע לִכְשֶׁאָמוּת, וְאֵין גֵּט לְאַחַר מִיתָה:


זה גטך אם מתי, לא אמר כלום – that implies, when I will die, but there is no Jewish bill of divorce after death.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 7:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/7:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/7:3:2)


**מֵחֹלִי זֶה.** מַשְׁמַע מֵחֹלִי זֶה וָאֵילָךְ, וְכֵיוָן שֶׁמֵּת מִתּוֹךְ הַחֹלִי נִמְצָא שֶׁאֵין הַגֵּט חָל אֶלָּא לְאַחַר מִיתָה:


מחולי זה – implying – from this illness and onwards, for since he died in the midst of this illness, the Jewish bill of divorce would not take effect until after death.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 7:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/7:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/7:3:3)


**מֵהַיּוֹם וּלְאַחַר מִיתָה גֵּט וְאֵינוֹ גֵט.** מְסָפְקָא לָן אִי תְנָאָה הָוֵי מֵהַיּוֹם אִם אָמוּת, וְכֵיוָן שֶׁמֵּת נִתְקַיֵּם הַתְּנַאי וְנִמְצָא שֶׁהוּא גֵּט מִשְּׁעַת נְתִינָתוֹ. אִי חֲזָרָה הָוֵי שֶׁחָזַר בּוֹ מִמַּאי דְּאָמַר מֵהַיּוֹם, וְאָמַר לְאַחַר מִיתָה יְהֵא גֵט, וְאֵינוֹ כְּלוּם כֵּיוָן דְּלֹא אָמַר מֵהַיּוֹם אִם מַתִּי:


מהיום ולאחר מילה גט ואינו גט – we doubt if he had made a condition to her, “from today if I die,” for when he died, the condition was fulfilled, and it follows that it was a Jewish bill of Divorce from the time he gave it [to her]; and if he retracted, it is that retracted from what he said “from today,” for he said, “after death it will be a Jewish bill of divorce,” and that is meaningless, since he did not say, “from today if I die.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 7:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/7:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/7:3:4)


**חוֹלֶצֶת.** שֶׁמָּא אֵינוֹ גֵט:


חולצת – lest it is not a Jewish bill of divorce


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 7:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/7:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/7:3:5)


**וְלֹא מִתְיַבֶּמֶת.** שֶׁמָּא גֵט הוּא וְהַוְיָא לַהּ גְּרוּשַׁת אָחִיו וְקָיְמָא עֲלֵיהּ בְּאִסּוּר כָּרֵת:


ולא מתיבמת – lest it was a Jewish divorce, and she would thereby the divorcee of his brother, we establish that she is subject to a prohibition which would involve extirpation [for it being violated].



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 7:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/7:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/7:4:1)


**לֹא תִתְיַחֵד עִמּוֹ.** זֶה שֶׁנָּתַן גֵּט וְאָמַר לָהּ מֵהַיּוֹם אִם מַתִּי, לֹא תִתְיַחֵד עִמּוֹ, שֶׁמָּא יָבֹא עָלֶיהָ וּתְהֵא צְרִיכָה גֵט שֵׁנִי, דְּחָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא בָּעַל לְשֵׁם קִדּוּשִׁין:


לא תתיחד עמו – This one who gave [her] a Jewish bill of divorce and said to her, “from today if I die,” she should not be alone with him, lest that he should come up upon her and she would need a second Jewish bill of divorce, for we are concerned lest he engaged in a sexual act with her for the purposes of betrothal.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Gittin 7:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/7:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Gittin/r/7:4:2)
